# agent_1: the whole run

[user message]
For each integer \(n\ge2\), let \(F_n\) be the free group on \(n\) generators. For any group \(G\), define its lower central series by \(\gamma_1(G)=G\) and \(\gamma_{r+1}(G)=[\gamma_r(G),G]\), where \([H,K]\) is the subgroup generated by the commutators \([h,k]=hkh^{-1}k^{-1}\) with \(h\in H\) and \(k\in K\). For every integer \(r\ge1\), define
\[
\mathcal A_r(n)=\ker\!\left(\operatorname{Aut}(F_n)\longrightarrow\operatorname{Aut}\bigl(F_n/\gamma_{r+1}(F_n)\bigr)\right),
\qquad \operatorname{IA}_n=\mathcal A_1(n).
\]
Determine, by explicit conditions on \(n\) and \(c\), the complete set
\[
\mathcal D=\left\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}:\ 
\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq
\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)
\right\}.
\]

The answer is \mathcal D=\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}: n\ge3,\ c\ge6\}. This was proved recently, so it is not an open problem: your team's task is to prove it, completely, and to write Python that really computes or checks every step that can be checked. Printing what you believe is not evidence. Arriving at any other answer is not acceptable, and neither is conceding: when an approach fails, try another.

Proving it takes two halves: for every \(n\ge3\) and \(c\ge6\), \(\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)\), and for every \(n\ge2\) and \(c\ge1\) with \(n=2\) or \(c\le5\), \(\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)=\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)\). Split the work, and the pieces of each part, between you on Slack.

Stop only when the team's proof is complete. Before you stop, post on Slack what you proved, and what is left.

#14 agent_4:
Rank2 proof complete modulo classical Nielsen/Magnus–Witt facts. Magnus–Witt identifies grγ(F_n) with free Lie subring L⊂Z⟨X1,…,Xn⟩. Its positive-degree center is zero: a homogeneous associative polynomial commuting with X1 must be a multiple of X1^d (rotate leading X1's in each word's coefficient until reaching a word starting with another letter, which must have coefficient zero); commuting with X2 then forces zero. Thus if [g,F_n]⊂γ_{c+2} but g∉γ_{c+1}, take r≤c with g∈γr\γr+1; its nonzero degree-r leading class commutes with all Xi, contradiction. Hence Z(F_n/γ_{c+2})=γ_{c+1}/γ_{c+2}. Since IA2=InnF2≅F2 and γc Inn=Inn γc, γc(IA2)∩A_{c+1}=Inn γ_{c+1}=γ_{c+1}(IA2), every c. Exact free-word certificates are workspace/rank2_certificate.py.

from itertools import product
from pathlib import Path

def red(w):
    out=[]
    for a in w:
        if out and out[-1]==-a: out.pop()
        else: out.append(a)

def inv(w): return tuple(-a for a in w[::-1])

def apply(f,w): return mul(*(f[a-1] if a>0 else inv(f[-a-1]) for a in w))

"""Exact degree-one Johnson-map check for Magnus generators.
No theorem about degrees >=2 is asserted by this computation.
"""
from itertools import combinations

def multiply(a,b,degree=2):
    c={}
    for u,x in a.items():
        for v,y in b.items():
            if len(u)+len(v)<=degree:
                w=u+v;c[w]=c.get(w,0)+x*y
    return {w:x for w,x in c.items() if x}

def expansion(word):
    out={():1}
    for s in word:
        i=abs(s)-1
        factor={():1,(i,):1} if s>0 else {():1,(i,):-1,(i,i):1}
        out=multiply(out,factor)
    return out

def generators(n):
    # Return (name, changed index, image word) with indices 0-based.
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            if i!=j:
                yield ('K',i,j),i,(j+1,i+1,-j-1)
        for j,k in combinations([j for j in range(n) if j!=i],2):
            yield ('C',i,j,k),i,(i+1,j+1,k+1,-j-1,-k-1)

def check(n):
    basis={(i,j,k) for i in range(n) for j,k in combinations(range(n),2)}
    images=[]
    for name,i,word in generators(n):
        d=expansion(word+(-i-1,));d[()]-=1
        d={u:x for u,x in d.items() if x}
        assert d and all(len(u)==2 for u in d)
        nonzero=[]
        for j,k in combinations(range(n),2):
            a=d.get((j,k),0)
            assert d.get((k,j),0)==-a
            if a: nonzero.append(((i,j,k),a))
        assert len(nonzero)==1
        key,sgn=nonzero[0]
        assert sgn in (-1,1)
        assert d=={(key[1],key[2]):sgn,(key[2],key[1]):-sgn}
        images.append(key)
    assert len(images)==len(set(images))==len(basis)==n*n*(n-1)//2
    assert set(images)==basis
    return len(images)

if __name__=='__main__':
    for n in range(2,13):
        print(n,check(n),'signed-permutation columns checked')

# Rank two and the structural reduction

Use composition f g = f after g. The classical Nielsen generator theorem says Aut(F_2) is generated by permutation, inversion, and elementary multiplication of a free basis. Set S=(b,a^{-1}), T=(a,ab), R=(a,b^{-1}); these generate the Nielsen generators. The classical presentation

GL_2(Z)=<S,T,R | S^4=1, (ST)^3=S^2, R^2=1, RSR=S^{-1}, RTR=T^{-1}>

has these abelianization matrices. Exact reduction of free words, checked in rank2_certificate.py, gives S^4=R^2=RSRS=1, (ST)^3 S^{-2}=Inn(ba^{-1}), RTRT=Inn(a). Thus the presentation gives a surjection GL_2(Z) -> Out(F_2), inverse to abelianization. Consequently IA_2=Inn(F_2).

For n>=2, Z(F_n/gamma_{c+2})=gamma_{c+1}/gamma_{c+2}. Indeed Magnus--Witt identifies gr_gamma(F_n) with the free Lie ring embedded in the free associative ring Z<X_1,...,X_n>. A positive-degree homogeneous associative polynomial P commuting with X_1 is a multiple of X_1^d: coefficients of a word w beginning in a different letter vanish by comparison in PX_1=X_1P, and initial X_1 letters can be cyclically moved to the end without changing coefficients. Commutation with X_2 forces that multiple zero. If g not in gamma_{c+1}, choose r<=c with g in gamma_r minus gamma_{r+1}; if [g,F_n] is contained in gamma_{c+2}, its nonzero initial form would commute with all X_i, contradiction. The reverse inclusion follows from the definition of gamma.

Since F_2 is centerless, conjugation identifies IA_2 with F_2 and gamma_c(IA_2) with Inn(gamma_c(F_2)). By the center formula, Inn(g) belongs to A_{c+1}(2) iff g belongs to gamma_{c+1}(F_2). Hence gamma_c(IA_2) intersect A_{c+1}(2) = gamma_{c+1}(IA_2) for every c.

For arbitrary n, work in the holomorph F_n semidirect Aut(F_n). The three-subgroup lemma, by induction on t, gives [A_r,gamma_t(F_n)] contained in gamma_{r+t}(F_n): for the induction step apply it to (A_r,gamma_t,F_n) modulo gamma_{r+t+1}; the other two iterated brackets vanish by induction and [gamma_i,gamma_j] contained in gamma_{i+j}. Applying the same lemma to (A_r,A_s,F_n) modulo gamma_{r+s+1} gives [A_r,A_s] contained in A_{r+s}. Thus gamma_c(IA_n) is contained in A_c. Define J_c on gamma_c(IA_n) by J_c(phi)(x mod gamma_2) = phi(x)x^{-1} mod gamma_{c+2}. It is a homomorphism into Hom(H,L_{c+1}) and vanishes on gamma_{c+1}(IA_n). Its induced graded map has kernel exactly (gamma_c(IA_n) intersect A_{c+1}) / gamma_{c+1}(IA_n). Thus the question is exactly whether this integral graded Johnson map has nonzero kernel in each bidegree.

Dependencies used here: Nielsen generation and the displayed modular-group presentation; Magnus--Witt's theorem. The novel theorem requested in ranks >=3 and degrees >=2 is not proved by these classical facts.

"""An exact integral proof certificate for degree-2 injectivity for IA_3.
Dependencies: Magnus generation and the degree-one Johnson homomorphism.
Relations used are genuine commuting pairs and their Nielsen conjugates.
"""
import sys
from itertools import combinations,product,permutations
from sympy import Matrix,ZZ
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
from degree1_certificate import generators,expansion

def red(w):
 s=[]
 for x in w:
  if s and s[-1]==-x:s.pop()
  else:s.append(x)
 return tuple(s)
def inv(w):return tuple(-x for x in reversed(w))
def compose(f,g):
 return tuple(red(y for x in w for y in (f[x-1] if x>0 else inv(f[-x-1]))) for w in g)
def add(a,b,scale=1):
 a=a.copy()
 for w,v in b.items():a[w]=a.get(w,0)+scale*v
 return {w:v for w,v in a.items() if v}
def deriv(d,p):
 o={}
 for w,v in p.items():
  for t,i in enumerate(w):
   for u,x in d[i].items():
    key=w[:t]+u+w[t+1:];o[key]=o.get(key,0)+v*x
 return {w:v for w,v in o.items() if v}
def certificate(n=3):
 ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));gs=[];ds=[]
 for _,i,w in generators(n):
  g=list(ident);g[i]=w;gs.append(tuple(g))
  p=add(expansion(w+(-i-1,)),{():1},-1)
  d=[{} for _ in range(n)];d[i]=p;ds.append(d)
 m=len(gs);pairs=list(combinations(range(m),2));bp={p:i for i,p in enumerate(pairs)}
 cols=[]
 for i,j in pairs:
  D=[add(deriv(ds[i],ds[j][k]),deriv(ds[j],ds[i][k]),-1) for k in range(n)]
  cols.append([D[k].get(w,0) for k in range(n) for w in product(range(n),repeat=3)])
 B=Matrix(cols).T
 rankB=B.rank()
 print('n',n,'m',m,'bracket rank',rankB,'null',len(pairs)-rankB,flush=True)
 cand=[]
 for i,g in enumerate(gs):cand.append((g,{i:1},('g',i)))
 for i,j in combinations(range(m),2):
  cand.append((compose(gs[i],gs[j]),{i:1,j:1},('p',i,j)))
  cand.append((compose(gs[j],gs[i]),{i:1,j:1},('p',j,i)))
 R=[];seen=set();cert=[]
 for (a,u,namea),(b,v,nameb) in combinations(cand,2):
  if compose(a,b)!=compose(b,a):continue
  r=[0]*len(pairs)
  for i,y in u.items():
   for j,x in v.items():
    if i<j:r[bp[i,j]]+=x*y
    if i>j:r[bp[j,i]]-=x*y
  t=tuple(r)
  if any(r) and t not in seen:
   seen.add(t);R.append(r);cert.append((namea,nameb))
 M=Matrix(R).T
 print('relations',len(R),'rank',M.rank(),flush=True)
 assert B*M==Matrix.zeros(B.rows,M.cols)
 keys=[];signs=[]
 for D in ds:
  k=next(k for k,p in enumerate(D) if p)
  w=next(w for w in D[k] if w[0]<w[1])
  keys.append((k,w));signs.append(D[k][w])
 actions=[]
 for ii,jj in permutations(range(n),2):
  t=list(ident);ti=list(ident)
  t[ii]=(ii+1,jj+1);ti[ii]=(ii+1,-jj-1)
  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  C=[]
  for g in gs:
   h=compose(t,compose(g,ti))
   hd=[add(expansion(h[k]+(-k-1,)),{():1},-1) for k in range(n)]
   C.append([hd[k].get(w,0)*sgn for (k,w),sgn in zip(keys,signs)])
  A=Matrix(C).T
  W=Matrix([[A[i,p]*A[j,q]-A[i,q]*A[j,p] for p,q in pairs] for i,j in pairs])
  actions.append(W)
 M=Matrix.hstack(M,*[W*M for W in actions])
 rankM=M.rank()
 print('GL-closure relations',M.cols,'rank',rankM,flush=True)
 assert B*M==Matrix.zeros(B.rows,M.cols)
 S=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ)
 diag=[abs(S[i,i]) for i in range(min(S.shape)) if S[i,i]]
 print('SNF',diag,flush=True)
 assert rankM==len(pairs)-rankB
 assert all(x==1 for x in diag)
 print('PASS: actual group relations equal the full integral bracket kernel')
 return B,M,cert

if __name__=='__main__':certificate(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 3)

--- stdout ---
"""An exact integral proof certificate for degree-2 injectivity for IA_3.
Dependencies: Magnus generation and the degree-one Johnson homomorphism.
Relations used are genuine commuting pairs and their Nielsen conjugates.
"""
import sys
from itertools import combinations,product,permutations
from sympy import Matrix,ZZ
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form, hermite_normal_form
from degree1_certificate import generators,expansion

def red(w):
 s=[]
 for x in w:
  if s and s[-1]==-x:s.pop()
  else:s.append(x)
 return tuple(s)
def inv(w):return tuple(-x for x in reversed(w))
def compose(f,g):
 return tuple(red(y for x in w for y in (f[x-1] if x>0 else inv(f[-x-1]))) for w in g)
def add(a,b,scale=1):
 a=a.copy()
 for w,v in b.items():a[w]=a.get(w,0)+scale*v
 return {w:v for w,v in a.items() if v}
def deriv(d,p):
 o={}
 for w,v in p.items():
  for t,i in enumerate(w):
   for u,x in d[i].items():
    key=w[:t]+u+w[t+1:];o[key]=o.get(key,0)+v*x
 return {w:v for w,v in o.items() if v}
def certificate(n=3):
 ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(n));gs=[];ds=[]
 for _,i,w in generators(n):
  g=list(ident);g[i]=w;gs.append(tuple(g))
  p=add(expansion(w+(-i-1,)),{():1},-1)
  d=[{} for _ in range(n)];d[i]=p;ds.append(d)
 m=len(gs);pairs=list(combinations(range(m),2));bp={p:i for i,p in enumerate(pairs)}
 cols=[]
 for i,j in pairs:
  D=[add(deriv(ds[i],ds[j][k]),deriv(ds[j],ds[i][k]),-1) for k in range(n)]
  cols.append([D[k].get(w,0) for k in range(n) for w in product(range(n),repeat=3)])
 B=Matrix(cols).T
 rankB=B.rank()
 print('n',n,'m',m,'bracket rank',rankB,'null',len(pairs)-rankB,flush=True)
 cand=[]
 for i,g in enumerate(gs):cand.append((g,{i:1},('g',i)))
 for i,j in combinations(range(m),2):
  cand.append((compose(gs[i],gs[j]),{i:1,j:1},('p',i,j)))
  cand.append((compose(gs[j],gs[i]),{i:1,j:1},('p',j,i)))
 R=[];seen=set();cert=[]
 for (a,u,namea),(b,v,nameb) in combinations(cand,2):
  if compose(a,b)!=compose(b,a):continue
  r=[0]*len(pairs)
  for i,y in u.items():
   for j,x in v.items():
    if i<j:r[bp[i,j]]+=x*y
    if i>j:r[bp[j,i]]-=x*y
  t=tuple(r)
  if any(r) and t not in seen:
   seen.add(t);R.append(r);cert.append((namea,nameb))
 M=Matrix(R).T
 print('relations',len(R),'rank',M.rank(),flush=True)
 assert B*M==Matrix.zeros(B.rows,M.cols)
 keys=[];signs=[]
 for D in ds:
  k=next(k for k,p in enumerate(D) if p)
  w=next(w for w in D[k] if w[0]<w[1])
  keys.append((k,w));signs.append(D[k][w])
 actions=[]
 for ii,jj in permutations(range(n),2):
  t=list(ident);ti=list(ident)
  t[ii]=(ii+1,jj+1);ti[ii]=(ii+1,-jj-1)
  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  C=[]
  for g in gs:
   h=compose(t,compose(g,ti))
   hd=[add(expansion(h[k]+(-k-1,)),{():1},-1) for k in range(n)]
   C.append([hd[k].get(w,0)*sgn for (k,w),sgn in zip(keys,signs)])
  A=Matrix(C).T
  W=Matrix([[A[i,p]*A[j,q]-A[i,q]*A[j,p] for p,q in pairs] for i,j in pairs])
  actions.append(W)
 M=Matrix.hstack(M,*[W*M for W in actions])
 rankM=M.rank()
 print('GL-closure relations',M.cols,'rank',rankM,flush=True)
 for roundno in range(2,5):
  if rankM==len(pairs)-rankB:break
  H=hermite_normal_form(M)
  assert H.cols==rankM
  M=Matrix.hstack(H,*[W*H for W in actions])
  newrank=M.rank()
  print('GL-closure round',roundno,'relations',M.cols,'rank',newrank,flush=True)
  if newrank==rankM:break
  rankM=newrank
 assert B*M==Matrix.zeros(B.rows,M.cols)
 S=smith_normal_form(M,domain=ZZ)
 diag=[abs(S[i,i]) for i in range(min(S.shape)) if S[i,i]]
 print('SNF',diag,flush=True)
 assert rankM==len(pairs)-rankB
 assert all(x==1 for x in diag)
 print('PASS: actual group relations equal the full integral bracket kernel')
 return B,M,cert

if __name__=='__main__':certificate(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 3)

# All-rank degree-two injectivity: weight classification audit

Uses the exact rank-3/rank-4 lattice certificate and the two universal
relations proved in `agent4_degree2_relations.md`.
Write a(i;jk) = e_i^* tensor [X_j,X_k], with j != k and antisymmetry
in j,k. Each is the signed Johnson image of one Magnus generator.
Call it K-type if i is j or k, and C-type otherwise. K-type has
weight e_t; C-type has weight e_j+e_k-e_i.

The abelian group Lambda^2(V) is weight graded. All relations below
are genuine relations in gr_2(IA_n). Its bracket map is integral.
For <=4 labels, any relation with zero derivation image is a genuine
relation by the exact n=4 certificate (and n=2/3 results).

Universal relations:
1. Same-target C-type pairs vanish in gr_2 (partial-inner identity).
2. Dummy collapse: a(i;pk) wedge a(p;qr) equals
   a(i;qk) wedge a(q;qr) when the initial and cross terms commute.
   This follows by shearing X_p -> X_p+X_q in the commuting relation
   a(i;pk) wedge a(q;qr)=0 and subtracting commuting terms.
   Besides the five-distinct-label case, it remains valid when k=i,
   provided i,p,q,r are distinct. If k=q choose r instead of q.

## Weights with a negative entry
Two distinct negative entries: neither target occurs in the opposite
tail, so the automorphisms commute exactly. An entry -2 means two
C-types with the same target, covered by relation 1.

Exactly one negative entry -e_i leaves three positive output labels
(counted with multiplicity). The derivation image has only target i
and is an integral degree-three free Lie element on those labels.
Any intermediate contracted target outside these labels is eliminated
by dummy collapse. Of q,r at least one differs from k because q!=r,
so an allowed collapse always exists. All remaining columns of any
fixed weight are supported on i plus the positive labels, at most four
labels. Consequently every kernel relation is a local rank<=4 relation.

## Weight e_a+e_b with a!=b
For each s outside {a,b}, put
 U_s=e_s^* [[s,a],b], V_s=e_s^* [[s,b],a],
 Z_a=e_a^* [a,[a,b]], Z_b=e_b^* [[a,b],b].
Use the following actual degree-two bracket columns:
 W_s=a(s;sa) wedge a(s;sb), image U_s-V_s;
 L_s=a(s;ab) wedge a(a;as), image U_s+Z_a;
 G=a(a;ab) wedge a(b;ab), image -Z_a+Z_b.
Their images are integrally independent: inspecting U_s,V_s forces
all W_s,L_s coefficients to vanish, and then inspecting Z_b kills G.

All K-K pairs either commute exactly or have support<=3.
A C-K pair must be a(i;ab) wedge K(p;i). If p lies outside {a,b,i},
dummy collapse (the allowed k=target case) replaces it by a bracket
on {a,b,p}; otherwise it already has support<=3.
A C-C pair must be
 E_ip=a(i;pa) wedge a(p;ib), with i,p outside {a,b} and i!=p.
Its image is -V_i+U_p, hence by rank4 injectivity
 E_ip=-L_i+L_p+W_i.
Every column supported on {a,b,s} has image in the primitive lattice
spanned by W_s,L_s,G: their 3 columns have a unit 3x3 minor in the
4-dimensional coordinate group (U_s,V_s,Z_a,Z_b). They span the
rank3 bracket image there, by the certified rank3 image computation.
Thus every column reduces integrally to W_s,L_s,G, whose independent
images prove injectivity.

## Weight 2e_a
K-K pairs commute (same conjugator). C-K is impossible because the
C-type has two distinct positive output labels. The only C-C columns
are E_ip=a(i;pa) wedge a(p;ia), i,p!=a, i!=p.
Put T_i=e_i^* [[i,a],a]. Their images are T_p-T_i.
Fix t!=a; the columns E_it (i!=a,t) have independent images.
Every E_ip=E_it+E_tp follows from rank4 injectivity on {a,i,p,t}.
Thus there is no kernel in this weight either.

These exhaust weights (sum of coordinates is two), proving
ker[Lambda^2(V)->Der_2]=relations in gr_2(IA_n), for every n.
Consequently gamma_2(IA_n) intersect A_3(n)=gamma_3(IA_n).
Together with the degree-one theorem this also gives A_3=gamma_3.

# Integral degree-two Johnson injectivity in every rank

## Inputs

Use the proved degree-one identification IA_n^ab=V_n=Hom(H,L_2), with signed basis a_i;jk=e_i^* tensor [X_j,X_k], j!=k. For i in {j,k} its lift is a signed basis conjugation, otherwise a commutator transvection. Let q:Lambda^2 V_n -> gr_2(IA_n) be the surjective bracket map and B:Lambda^2 V_n -> Hom(H,L_3) the derivation bracket, so B=J_2 q.

The exact primitive-lattice certificates degree2_certificate.py prove J_2 injective for n=3,4; rank2 follows from rank2_proof.md. Consequently any z supported on at most four coordinate labels with Bz=0 has qz=0 in every rank: embed the corresponding smaller free-group automorphism group by fixing the extra generators. No injectivity of this group inclusion on lower-central quotients is required.

## Reduction of individual columns

The degree-one basis is multihomogeneous with weight e_j+e_k-e_i; hence Lambda^2 V is a direct sum of integral weight groups and B preserves weight. This is an algebraic multigrading; no assumption that weight-space decomposition follows from the integral diagonal torus is made.

Consider a wedge of two basis derivations. With different targets and no target occurring in the other's output pair, their Magnus lifts commute exactly. With the same target, if both are commutator transvections, their wedge is a true degree-two relation in all ranks by the partial-inner identity proved in agent4_degree2_relations.md. Every remaining column has at least one cross-contraction and its union support has at most five labels. A column supported on exactly five labels has two distinct-target commutator transvections and exactly one contraction; all five labels are distinct and it has form a_i;pk wedge a_p;qr, up to swapping factors/signs. The integral dummy-exchange relation from agent4_degree2_relations.md replaces it by a_i;qk wedge a_q;qr, supported on four labels. Thus every original column is, modulo true relations in ker q, zero or a column supported on at most four labels.

We now canonicalize each resulting column using only relations supported on at most four labels. It suffices to do this weight by weight. The possible nonzero weights in Hom(H,L_3) are (I) exactly one coefficient -1, all others nonnegative; (II) 2e_a; (III) e_a+e_b, a!=b. For any other weight B is zero and every reduced column is a local relation, hence q-zero.

## I: One negative coefficient

Write the weight as -e_i plus three positive letters with multiplicity, none equal to i. The output space is the corresponding multihomogeneous component of the free Lie ring on these positive letters, at target i. If the three letters are distinct a,b,c, an integral basis is [[a,b],c], [[a,c],b]. Both are images, up to sign, of local columns a_i;a c wedge a_a;a b and a_i;a b wedge a_a;a c. If the letters are a,a,b, the output is rank one, generated by [a,[a,b]], which is the image up to sign of a_i;a b wedge a_b;a b. Three identical letters give zero. Choose these columns as canonical lifts of a free basis.

For any reduced column, express its image in this integral basis. Subtract the corresponding canonical lifts. The difference is supported on at most four labels: the actual weight support has size four in the distinct-letter case and size three in the repeated-letter case, while the reduced column has at most four labels and contains the actual weight support. Its image is zero, so local injectivity proves the required equality in gr_2. The canonical images are independent. Therefore J_2 is injective on this weight.

## II: Weight 2e_a

The codomain weight has basis T_i=e_i^*[[X_i,X_a],X_a], i!=a. Every K--K column of this weight consists of two conjugations by X_a and commutes exactly. Every remaining nonzero column has form E_ip=a_i;p a wedge a_p;i a, with i,p distinct from a; its image is -T_i+T_p. Fix any anchor j!=a and take E_ji as canonical star columns, i!=a,j. Their images T_i-T_j are independent. The difference E_ip-(E_jp-E_ji) has zero image and is supported on {a,i,p,j}, at most four labels; terms with repeated indices are interpreted as zero and skew signs. Therefore local injectivity expresses every column in these independent star columns. J_2 is injective on this weight.

## III: Weight e_a+e_b, a!=b

For s outside {a,b}, put U_s=e_s^*[[X_s,X_a],X_b] and V_s=e_s^*[[X_s,X_b],X_a]. Put Z_a=e_a^*[X_a,[X_a,X_b]] and Z_b=e_b^*[[X_a,X_b],X_b]. These form an integral codomain basis. Use the actual columns

W_s=a_s;s a wedge a_s;s b,
L_s=a_s;a b wedge a_a;a s,
G=a_a;a b wedge a_b;a b.

Their images are respectively U_s-V_s, U_s+Z_a, and -Z_a+Z_b. These images are independent and form the full integral kernel of the functional sending every U_s,V_s to +1 and Z_a,Z_b to -1. This follows directly by solving coefficients at U_s,V_s, then at Z_a,Z_b; equivalently the corresponding minors have gcd one.

This functional is the symmetric trace on this weight. Define trace on an associative expansion of D(X_t) by contracting its first letter with t and symmetrizing the remaining two letters. It gives

Tr(e_t^*[[u,v],w])=delta_tu vw-delta_tv uw,
Tr(e_t^*[u,[v,w]])=-delta_tv uw+delta_tw uv.

Substitution into the derivation-bracket formula shows Tr([D,E])=0 for any degree-one derivations D,E; cancellation is termwise. The script agent4_trace_certificate.py verifies this exactly for every pair of degree-one basis derivations through n=8 (41,250 pairs), as well as all W_s,L_s,G signs. The displayed formulas give the general proof independently of these finite tests.

For a reduced column supported on T, with |T|<=4 and {a,b} subset T, its trace-zero image therefore has a unique integral expression in G and those W_s,L_s for s in T minus {a,b}. Subtract that expression from the column. The result has zero image and support within T, so local rank<=4 injectivity makes it a true relation. Thus all columns are expressed in the globally independent G,W_s,L_s. J_2 is injective on this weight.

## Conclusion

Every integral weight has zero Johnson kernel, hence J_2:gr_2(IA_n)->Hom(H,L_3) is injective for every n>=2. Equivalently gamma_2(IA_n) intersect A_3(n)=gamma_3(IA_n). Combined with the degree-one theorem A_2=gamma_2, this proves A_3(n)=gamma_3(IA_n) for all n.

This establishes only c=2 of the requested all-rank low-degree range. It does not establish c=3,4,5 or any high-degree noninjectivity.

PASS exact symmetric-trace cancellation for 41250 degree-one derivation pairs (n=2..8)
PASS exact W_s,L_s,G derivative formulas

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/agent1_degree3.py')
p.write_text('''"""Exact exploratory degree-three relation computation for IA3.
Every relation column is justified by an actual exact commuting identity.
PASS only if full primitive Johnson kernel is obtained.
"""
from itertools import product, combinations
from functools import lru_cache
from sage.all import Matrix, ZZ
from degree1_certificate import generators
from degree2_certificate import red, inv, compose

N=3
G=[]
for name,i,w in generators(N):
 f=[(j+1,) for j in range(N)];f[i]=w;G.append(tuple(f))
m=len(G)

def mul(a,b,lim=3):
 out={}
 for u,x in a.items():
  for v,y in b.items():
   if len(u)+len(v)<=lim:out[u+v]=out.get(u+v,0)+x*y
 return {w:x for w,x in out.items() if x}
def add(a,b,k=1):
 out=a.copy()
 for w,x in b.items():out[w]=out.get(w,0)+k*x
 return {w:x for w,x in out.items() if x}
def br(a,b):return add(mul(a,b,20),mul(b,a,20),-1)
def expand(w):
 a={():1}
 for s in w:
  i=abs(s)-1
  f={():1,(i,):1} if s>0 else {tuple([i]*j):(-1)**j for j in range(4)}
  a=mul(a,f)
 return a

def commuting_relators():
 cand=[]
 for i,g in enumerate(G):cand.append((g,(i+1,)))
 for i,j in combinations(range(m),2):
  cand.append((compose(G[i],G[j]),(i+1,j+1)))
  cand.append((compose(G[j],G[i]),(j+1,i+1)))
 seen=set();out=[]
 for (a,u),(b,v) in combinations(cand,2):
  if compose(a,b)!=compose(b,a):continue
  w=red(u+v+inv(u)+inv(v));ex=expand(w)
  if any(len(t)==1 and x for t,x in ex.items()):raise ValueError('bad')
  key=tuple(sorted((t,x) for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==2))
  if not key or key in seen:continue
  seen.add(key);out.append((w,ex))
 return out

def lyndon(w):return bool(w) and all(w < w[j:]+w[:j] for j in range(1,len(w)))
@lru_cache(None)
def lp(w):
 if len(w)==1:return {w:1}
 j=next(j for j in range(1,len(w)) if lyndon(w[j:]))
 return br(lp(w[:j]),lp(w[j:]))
L3=[w for w in product(range(m),repeat=3) if lyndon(w)]
LP=[lp(w) for w in L3]
def coords(p):
 p=p.copy();v=[]
 for w,q in zip(L3,LP):
  x=p.get(w,0);v.append(x)
  if x:p=add(p,q,-x)
 assert not p,('not free Lie',p)
 return v

def deriv(d,p):
 out={}
 for w,x in p.items():
  for j,t in enumerate(w):
   for v,y in d[t].items():out[w[:j]+v+w[j+1:]]=out.get(w[:j]+v+w[j+1:],0)+x*y
 return {w:x for w,x in out.items() if x}
def dbr(d,e):return tuple(add(deriv(d,e[i]),deriv(e,d[i]),-1) for i in range(N))
Ds=[]
for name,i,w in generators(N):
 # Calculate degree-two displacement using the same generic Magnus routine.
 ex=expand(w+(-i-1,));d=[{} for _ in range(N)]
 d[i]={t:x for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==2};Ds.append(tuple(d))
@lru_cache(None)
def ld(w):
 if len(w)==1:return Ds[w[0]]
 j=next(j for j in range(1,len(w)) if lyndon(w[j:]))
 return dbr(ld(w[:j]),ld(w[j:]))

def main():
 rel=commuting_relators();print('actual commuting relators',len(rel),flush=True)
 pairs=list(combinations(range(m),2))
 R2=Matrix(ZZ,[[ex.get((i,j),0) for w,ex in rel] for i,j in pairs])
 K=R2.right_kernel().basis_matrix();print('R2 rank',R2.rank(),'integer syzygies',K.nrows(),flush=True)
 cols=[]
 for w,ex in rel:
  q={t:x for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==2}
  for i in range(m):cols.append(coords(br({(i,):1},q)))
 for z in K.rows():
  p={}
  for x,(_,ex) in zip(z,rel):
   if x:p=add(p,{t:y for t,y in ex.items() if len(t)==3},x)
  if p:cols.append(coords(p))
 M=Matrix(ZZ,cols).transpose();print('cubic relation matrix',M.dimensions(),'rank',M.rank(),flush=True)
 keys=[(i,w) for i in range(N) for w in product(range(N),repeat=4)]
 B=Matrix(ZZ,[[ld(w)[i].get(t,0) for w in L3] for i,t in keys])
 print('domain',len(L3),'Johnson rank',B.rank(),'kernel',len(L3)-B.rank(),flush=True)
 assert B*M==0
 H=M.hermite_form(include_zero_rows=False) if False else None
 S=M.smith_form(transformation=False)
 diag=[abs(S[i,i]) for i in range(min(S.dimensions())) if S[i,i]]
 print('SNF counts',{int(x):diag.count(x) for x in set(diag)},flush=True)
 if len(diag)==len(L3)-B.rank() and all(x==1 for x in diag):
  print('PASS: full integral degree3 Johnson kernel generated by verified relations')
 else:print('INCOMPLETE relation lattice; no injectivity claim')
 return B,M,rel
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent1_degree3 import main
main()

Coordinates are degree2 and twice degree3 of log in exponential variables.
"""
import json
from itertools import combinations
from sage.all import Matrix, ZZ
from agent1_degree3 import *

pairs=list(combinations(range(m),2))
L2=[br({(i,):1},{(j,):1}) for i,j in pairs]
def coords2(p):
 v=[p.get((i,j),0) for i,j in pairs]
 z={}
 for x,q in zip(v,L2):z=add(z,q,x)
 assert z==p
 return v

def initial_vector(ex):
 r2={t:x for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==2}
 p3={t:2*x for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==3}
 for (i,j),x in r2.items():
  p3=add(p3,{(i,i,j):x,(i,j,j):x})
 return coords2(r2)+coords(p3)

def linear_sub(p,L):
 out={}
 for w,x in p.items():
  q={():x}
  for i in w:q=mul(q,L[i],3)
  out=add(out,q)
 return out

def action_matrix(words):
 L=[];Q=[]
 for w in words:
  ex=expand(w);li={t:x for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==1}
  f2={t:x for t,x in ex.items() if len(t)==2}
  qi=add({t:2*x for t,x in f2.items()},mul(li,li,3),-1)
  for (i,),x in li.items():qi=add(qi,{(i,i):x})
  coords2(qi)
  L.append(li);Q.append(qi)
 cols=[]
 for i,j in pairs:
  a=coords2(br(L[i],L[j]))
  b=coords(add(br(Q[i],L[j]),br(L[i],Q[j])))
  cols.append(a+b)
 for p in LP:cols.append([0]*len(pairs)+coords(linear_sub(p,L)))
 return Matrix(ZZ,cols).transpose()

def row_basis(M):
 return M.hermite_form(include_zero_rows=False)

def main():
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
 # Audit supplied action words against their exact automorphisms.
 from agent2_nielsen_actions import evaluate
 acts=[]
 for name,d in data['actions'].items():
  words=[tuple(w) for w in d['words']]
  # The source file independently checks these; audit again here.
  ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(N))
  ig=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in data['inverses']]
  def ev(w):
   f=ident
   for s in w:f=compose(f,G[s-1] if s>0 else ig[-s-1])
   return f
  t=tuple(map(tuple,d['auto']));ti=tuple(map(tuple,d['inverse']))
  assert compose(t,ti)==ident and compose(ti,t)==ident
  for g,w in zip(G,words):assert ev(w)==compose(t,compose(g,ti))
  A=action_matrix(words);acts.append(A)
  print('built and audited action',name,flush=True)
 rel=commuting_relators()
 H=row_basis(Matrix(ZZ,[initial_vector(ex) for w,ex in rel]))
 print('initial relation lattice rank',H.nrows(),flush=True)
 for r in range(1,9):
  parts=[H]+[H*A.transpose() for A in acts]
  extra=[]
  for z in H.rows():
   r2={}
   for x,q in zip(z[:len(pairs)],L2):r2=add(r2,q,x)
   if not r2:continue
   for i in range(m):extra.append([0]*len(pairs)+[2*x for x in coords(br({(i,):1},r2))])
  if extra:parts.append(Matrix(ZZ,extra))
  newH=row_basis(Matrix(ZZ,[list(z) for P in parts for z in P.rows()]))
  print('closure',r,'rank',newH.nrows(),flush=True)
  if H==newH:break
  H=newH
 R2=H[:,:len(pairs)]
 K=R2.transpose().right_kernel().basis_matrix()
 C=K*H[:,len(pairs):]
 assert all(x%2==0 for x in C.list())
 C=C.apply_map(lambda x:x//2)
 C=row_basis(C)
 print('quadratic rank',R2.rank(),'cubic relation rank',C.nrows(),flush=True)
 # Compute exact target matrix in Lyndon coordinates.
 keys=[(i,w) for i in range(N) for w in product(range(N),repeat=4)]
 B=Matrix(ZZ,[[ld(w)[i].get(t,0) for w in L3] for i,t in keys])
 assert B*C.transpose()==0
 S=C.smith_form(transformation=False)
 diag=[abs(S[i,i]) for i in range(min(S.dimensions())) if S[i,i]]
 print('Johnson rank',B.rank(),'kernel',len(L3)-B.rank(),'SNF counts',{int(x):diag.count(x) for x in set(diag)},flush=True)
 if C.nrows()==len(L3)-B.rank() and all(x==1 for x in diag):print('PASS: degree3 IA3 integral Johnson injectivity')
 else:print('INCOMPLETE: no injectivity claim')
 return H,C,B
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/agent1_ia_collisions.py')
p.write_text('''"""Exact IA3 relation search by equal reduced-word automorphism images."""
import json,time
from pathlib import Path
from agent1_degree3 import *
from agent1_degree3_closure import initial_vector,row_basis

def search(radius=4):
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
 IG=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in data['inverses']]
 ident=tuple((i+1,) for i in range(N))
 for g,h in zip(G,IG):assert compose(g,h)==ident and compose(h,g)==ident
 table={ident:()};front=[(ident,())];rels=[];seen=set();start=time.time()
 for r in range(1,radius+1):
  nxt=[]
  for f,w in front:
   for s in range(-m,m+1):
    if s==0 or (w and s==-w[-1]):continue
    h=compose(f,G[s-1] if s>0 else IG[-s-1]);v=w+(s,)
    if h in table:
     rel=red(v+inv(table[h]))
     if not rel:continue
     ex=expand(rel);z=tuple(initial_vector(ex))
     if not any(z) or z in seen or tuple(-x for x in z) in seen:continue
     seen.add(z);rels.append(rel)
    else:table[h]=v;nxt.append((h,v))
  front=nxt
  print('radius',r,'images',len(table),'frontier',len(front),'distinct nonzero class3 relations',len(rels),'seconds',round(time.time()-start,2),flush=True)
 path=Path('workspace/agent1_ia3_collision_relators.json');path.write_text(json.dumps(rels))
 print('saved',path,flush=True)
 return rels
if __name__=='__main__':search(4)
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')

rels=search(4)

import json
from functools import lru_cache
from itertools import permutations
from sage.all import Matrix, ZZ
from agent1_degree3 import *
from agent1_degree3_closure import initial_vector

ONE={():1}
ID=tuple({(i,):1} for i in range(m))
def tmul(a,b):
 out={}
 aa=[[] for _ in range(4)];bb=[[] for _ in range(4)]
 for w,x in a.items():aa[len(w)].append((w,x))
 for w,x in b.items():bb[len(w)].append((w,x))
 for r in range(4):
  for s in range(4-r):
   for u,x in aa[r]:
    for v,y in bb[s]:out[u+v]=out.get(u+v,0)+x*y
 return {w:x for w,x in out.items() if x}
def subst(p,Q,cache=None):
 if cache is None:cache={():ONE}
 def mon(w):
  if w not in cache:cache[w]=tmul(mon(w[:-1]),Q[w[-1]])
  return cache[w]
 out={}
 for w,x in p.items():out=add(out,mon(w),x)
 return out

def ec(E,F):
 cache={():ONE}
 return tuple(subst(p,E,cache) for p in F)
def ei(E):
 A=Matrix(ZZ,[[p.get((j,),0) for j in range(m)] for p in E])
 Ai=A.inverse();assert Ai.base_ring()==ZZ or all(x in ZZ for x in Ai.list())
 Q=tuple({(j,):int(Ai[i,j]) for j in range(m) if Ai[i,j]} for i in range(m))
 L=Q
 for d in (2,3):
  PQ=ec(E,Q);new=[]
  for i,p in enumerate(PQ):
   err={w:x for w,x in p.items() if len(w)==d}
   new.append(add(Q[i],subst(err,L),-1))
  Q=tuple(new)
 assert ec(E,Q)==ID and ec(Q,E)==ID
 return Q

def ew(words):return tuple(add(expand(w),ONE,-1) for w in words)
def inner(i):
 out=[]
 for h in range(m):out.append(add(expand((i+1,h+1,-i-1)),ONE,-1))
 return tuple(out)

def main():
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
 act={name:ew(d['words']) for name,d in data['actions'].items()}
 # Signed permutation action on IA basis is exact, not just linear.
 names=[tuple(x) for x in data['names']]
 @lru_cache(None)
 def perm(perm):
  words=[]
  for name in names:
   if name[0]=='K':target=('K',perm[name[1]],perm[name[2]]);sgn=1
   else:
    i,j,k=[perm[x] for x in name[1:]];sgn=1 if j<k else -1
    target=('C',i,min(j,k),max(j,k))
   words.append((sgn*(names.index(target)+1),))
  return ew(words)
 @lru_cache(None)
 def R(i,j):
  pm=(i,j)+tuple(t for t in range(N) if t not in (i,j))
  ip=tuple(pm.index(t) for t in range(N))
  return ec(perm(pm),ec(act['T'],perm(ip)))
 @lru_cache(None)
 def I(i):
  pm=(i,)+tuple(t for t in range(N) if t!=i)
  ip=tuple(pm.index(t) for t in range(N))
  return ec(perm(pm),ec(act['I'],perm(ip)))
 out=[]
 for gi,name in enumerate(names):
  if name[0]=='K':
   _,i,j=name;r=ei(R(i,j));e=ec(I(i),ec(r,ec(I(i),r)))
  else:
   _,i,j,k=name;a=R(i,j);b=R(i,k)
   e=ec(a,ec(b,ec(ei(a),ei(b))))
  for h,p in enumerate(e):
   # phi_g(h) g h^-1 g^-1 is an actual identity in IA.
   ex=tmul(add(p,ONE),expand((gi+1,-h-1,-gi-1)))
   assert all(not x for w,x in ex.items() if len(w)==1)
   out.append(initial_vector(ex))
  print('built Peiffer relators for',gi+1,name,flush=True)
 json.dump(out,open('workspace/agent1_peiffer_vectors_c3.json','w'))
 print('saved',len(out),'exact class3 Peiffer vectors',flush=True)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')

p.write_text('''import json,time
from sage.all import Matrix,ZZ
from agent1_degree3 import *
from agent1_degree3_closure import initial_vector,row_basis

def reduce(H,v):
 v=list(v)
 for row in H:
  p=next((j for j,x in enumerate(row) if x),None)
  if p is None:continue
  if v[p]:
   q=v[p]//row[p]
   if q:
    for j,x in enumerate(row):
     if x:v[j]-=q*x
 return v

def run(start=0,end=None):
 hp='workspace/agent1_class3_HNF215.json'
 H=json.load(open(hp));rels=json.load(open('workspace/agent1_ia3_collision_relators.json'))
 if end is None:end=len(rels)
 updates=0;t=time.time()
 for idx in range(start,min(end,len(rels))):
  w=rels[idx]
  v=initial_vector(expand(w));r=reduce(H,v)
  if any(r):
   H=[[int(x) for x in z] for z in row_basis(Matrix(ZZ,H+[r])).rows()]
   updates+=1;print('lattice enlarged at relation',idx,'update',updates,flush=True)
  if idx%5000==0:print('processed',idx,'seconds',round(time.time()-t,2),flush=True)
 json.dump(H,open(hp,'w'))
 print('done',start,end,'updates',updates,'seconds',round(time.time()-t,2),flush=True)
 return H
if __name__=='__main__':run()
''')

from agent1_degree3_closure import initial_vector,main

IG=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))['inverses']]
def commword(u,v):return red(tuple(u)+tuple(v)+inv(u)+inv(v))
rows=[];ident=tuple((j+1,) for j in range(N))
for i in range(N):
 a,b=[j for j in range(N) if j!=i]
 c=(names.index(('C',i,a,b))+1,)
 pa=(names.index(('K',b,a))+1,);pb=(names.index(('K',a,b))+1,)
 pw=commword(pa,pb)
 ts=[()]+[(s,) for s in (a+1,-a-1,b+1,-b-1)]+[w for w in itertools.product((a+1,-a-1,b+1,-b-1),repeat=2) if w[0]!=-w[1]]
 for t in ts:
  pt=tuple(z for s in t for z in ((pa if s>0 else inv(pa)) if abs(s)==a+1 else (pb if s>0 else inv(pb))))
  tv=pt+c+inv(pt);pv=pt+pw+inv(pt)
  rel=red(commword(tv,c)+inv(commword(pv,c)))
  f=ident
  for s in rel:f=compose(f,G[s-1] if s>0 else IG[-s-1])
  assert f==ident
  rows.append(initial_vector(expand(rel)))

from sage.all import Matrix,ZZ
from agent1_degree3 import *
from agent1_degree3_closure import row_basis

def lw(w):
 if len(w)==1:return (w[0]+1,)
 j=next(j for j in range(1,len(w)) if lyndon(w[j:]))
 a,b=lw(w[:j]),lw(w[j:]);return red(a+b+inv(a)+inv(b))

def main():
 H=Matrix(ZZ,json.load(open('workspace/agent1_class3_HNF215.json')))
 K=H[:,:36].transpose().right_kernel().basis_matrix()
 C=K*H[:,36:];assert all(x%2==0 for x in C.list())
 C=row_basis(C.apply_map(lambda x:x//2))
 S,U,V=C.smith_form();Vi=V.inverse()
 reps=[]
 for i in range(min(S.dimensions())):
  if abs(S[i,i])>1:reps.append((int(abs(S[i,i])),[int(x) for x in Vi[i]]))
 print('torsion reps',len(reps),'supports',[sum(x!=0 for x in v) for d,v in reps],flush=True)
 json.dump(reps,open('workspace/agent1_class3_torsion_reps.json','w'))
 IG=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))['inverses']]
 ident=tuple((j+1,) for j in range(N))
 for idx,(d,v) in enumerate(reps):

  for x,u in zip(v,L3):
   if x:w+=((lw(u) if x>0 else inv(lw(u)))*abs(x))
  w=red(w);f=ident
  for t in w:f=compose(f,G[t-1] if t>0 else IG[-t-1])
  print(idx,'order',d,'abstract length',len(w),'image lengths',list(map(len,f)),'IDENTITY',f==ident,flush=True)
 return reps
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')
import sys

main()

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/agent1_engel_search.py')
p.write_text('''import json,time
from itertools import product
from sage.all import Matrix,ZZ
from agent1_degree3 import *
from agent1_degree3_closure import row_basis
from agent1_collision_reduce import reduce

def main():
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
 IG=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in data['inverses']]
 ident=tuple((j+1,) for j in range(N))
 H=json.load(open('workspace/agent1_class3_HNF215.json'))
 cand=[]
 def ev(w):
  f=ident
  for s in w:f=compose(f,G[s-1] if s>0 else IG[-s-1])
  return f
 for ell in (1,2):
  for w in product(tuple(range(1,m+1))+tuple(range(-m,0)),repeat=ell):
   if red(w)!=w:continue
   a=[0]*m
   for s in w:a[abs(s)-1]+=1 if s>0 else -1
   if not any(a):continue
   cand.append((w,ev(w),ev(inv(w)),tuple(a)))
 cache={};eligible=0;hits=0;new=[];t=time.time()
 for ai,(wa,a,ia,u) in enumerate(cand):
  for wh,h,ih,v in cand:
   key=(u,v)
   if key not in cache:
    A={(i,):x for i,x in enumerate(u) if x};T={(i,):x for i,x in enumerate(v) if x}
    q=br(A,br(T,A))
    if not q:cache[key]=None;continue
    da=tuple({} for _ in range(N));dh=da
    da=tuple({} for _ in range(N));dh=tuple({} for _ in range(N))
    for i,x in enumerate(u):
     if x:da=tuple(add(p,q,x) for p,q in zip(da,Ds[i]))
    for i,x in enumerate(v):
     if x:dh=tuple(add(p,q,x) for p,q in zip(dh,Ds[i]))
    if any(dbr(da,dbr(dh,da))):cache[key]=None;continue
    z=[0]*36+[2*x for x in coords(q)]
    cache[key]=z if any(reduce(H,z)) else None
   z=cache[key]
   if z is None:continue
   eligible+=1;b=compose(h,compose(a,ih))
   if compose(a,b)!=compose(b,a):continue
   hits+=1;print('FOUND NEW ACTUAL ENGEL RELATION',wa,wh,flush=True)
   r=red(wa+wh+wa+inv(wh)+inv(wa)+wh+inv(wa)+inv(wh))
   assert ev(r)==ident
   from agent1_degree3_closure import initial_vector
   zactual=initial_vector(expand(r))
   assert zactual==z or zactual==[-x for x in z]
   new.append(zactual)
   H=[[int(x) for x in row] for row in row_basis(Matrix(ZZ,H+[zactual])).rows()]
   cache={}
  if ai%50==0:print('a',ai,'eligible pairs',eligible,'hits',hits,'seconds',round(time.time()-t,2),flush=True)
 json.dump(new,open('workspace/agent1_engel_vectors.json','w'))
 print('DONE eligible',eligible,'hits',hits,flush=True)
 return new
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')

main()

"""Finite exact Reidemeister-Schreier chunks; no completeness claim."""
import json,pickle
from pathlib import Path
from degree2_certificate import compose,inv,red
from agent2_nielsen_actions import magnus

def mat(f):return tuple(sum((1 if x==i+1 else -1 if x==-i-1 else 0) for x in f[j]) for i in range(3) for j in range(3))
def mm(a,b):return tuple(sum(a[3*i+k]*b[3*k+j] for k in range(3)) for i in range(3) for j in range(3))

def run(radius=4,mitm=3,start_radius=2,verbose=False):
 ar=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_AutF3_presentation.json'))
 A=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in ar['aut_images']];AI=[tuple(map(tuple,f)) for f in ar['inverse_images']]
 ident,gs,gis,names=magnus(3);mi=mat(ident)
 letters=[(i+1,g,AI[i],mat(g)) for i,g in enumerate(A)]+[(-i-1,AI[i],g,mat(AI[i])) for i,g in enumerate(A)]
 # Short GL transversal, with exact Aut lifts and inverse lifts.
 trans={mi:((),ident,ident)};front=[mi]
 for depth in range(1,radius+1):
  new=[]
  for M in front:
   w,f,fi=trans[M]
   for x,g,gi,Mg in letters:
    Q=mm(M,Mg)
    if Q in trans:continue
    trans[Q]=(w+(x,),compose(f,g),compose(gi,fi));new.append(Q)
  front=new;print('GLball',depth,len(trans),flush=True)
 # Magnus radius4 table.
 ll=[(i+1,g,gis[i]) for i,g in enumerate(gs)]+[(-i-1,gis[i],g) for i,g in enumerate(gs)]
 tab={ident:((),ident)};front=[ident];short=[]
 for depth in range(1,5):
  new=[]
  for f in front:
   w,fi=tab[f]
   for x,g,gi in ll:
    if w and w[-1]==-x:continue
    h=compose(f,g)
    if h in tab:continue
    tab[h]=(w+(x,),compose(gi,fi));new.append(h)
  front=new
  if depth<=mitm:short+=new
 print('IA radius4',len(tab),'complements',len(short),flush=True)
 cp=Path('workspace/agent2_rs_edgecache.pkl')
 cache=pickle.loads(cp.read_bytes()) if cp.exists() else {}
 fail=set();relations=[]
 starts=[M for M,(w,f,fi) in trans.items() if len(w)<=start_radius]
 def express(f):
  if f in cache:return cache[f]
  if f in fail:return None
  if f in tab:cache[f]=tab[f][0];return cache[f]
  for b in short:
   bw,bi=tab[b];a=compose(f,bi)
   if a in tab:
    cache[f]=red(tab[a][0]+bw);return cache[f]
  fail.add(f);return None
 def ev(w):
  f=ident
  for x in w:f=compose(f,gs[x-1] if x>0 else gis[-x-1])
  return f
 def al(x):return A[x-1] if x>0 else AI[-x-1]
 def ali(x):return AI[x-1] if x>0 else A[-x-1]
 # Build missing transversal vertices consistently from actual loop prefixes.
 loops=[tuple(reversed(r)) for r in ar['relators']]
 paths=[(start,ri,r) for start in starts for ri,r in enumerate(loops)]
 for start,ri,r in paths:
  w,f,fi=trans[start];M=start
  for x in r:
   M=mm(M,mat(al(x)));f=compose(f,al(x));fi=compose(ali(x),fi);w=w+(x,)
   if M not in trans:trans[M]=(w,f,fi)
  assert f==trans[start][1] and M==start
 for loopnum,(start,ri,r) in enumerate(paths):
  M=start;out=();okay=True
  for pos,x in enumerate(r):
   Q=mm(M,mat(al(x)));f=compose(trans[M][1],compose(al(x),trans[Q][2]))
   assert mat(f)==mi
   w=express(f)
   if w is None:
    
    if verbose:print('loop',loopnum,'edge',pos,'FAIL autlength',sum(map(len,f)),flush=True)
    okay=False;break
   out=red(out+w);M=Q
  if okay:
   assert ev(out)==ident
   if out:
    relations.append(out)
    
    if verbose:print('loop',loopnum,'SUCCESS IAwordlength',len(out),flush=True)
 Path('workspace/agent2_rs_relator_words.json').write_text(json.dumps(relations))
 cp.write_bytes(pickle.dumps(cache))
 print('Saved',len(relations),'exact RS identities; cache',len(cache),'fails',len(fail),flush=True)
 return relations
if __name__=='__main__':run()

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/agent1_rs_central_analysis.py')
p.write_text('''"""RS class3 upper bound allowing unknown central γ3 edge tails.
Depends on proved A3=γ3, checked degree2 certificate, and genuine AutF3 presentation.
"""
import json,time
import numpy as np
from sage.all import Matrix,ZZ,GF
from agent1_degree3 import expand,coords,L3,ld,N
from agent1_degree3_closure import row_basis
from agent1_peiffer_c3 import inv

M=9
ZERO=(np.zeros(M,dtype=np.int64),np.zeros((M,M),dtype=np.int64),np.zeros((M,M,M),dtype=np.int64))
def poly(w):
 p=expand(w);out=tuple(a.copy() for a in ZERO)
 for t,x in p.items():
  if t:out[len(t)-1][t]=int(x)
 return out

def pmul(P,Q):
 a,b,c=P;d,e,f=Q
 return (a+d,b+e+np.einsum('i,j->ij',a,d),c+f+np.einsum('i,jk->ijk',a,e)+np.einsum('ij,k->ijk',b,d))

class Eliminator:
 def __init__(self,p,projection,Bfree,needed):
  self.p=p;self.proj=projection;self.Bfree=Bfree;self.needed=needed;self.unknown={};self.known={};self.rows=0
 def known_insert(self,v):
  p=self.p
  while np.any(v):
   k=int(np.flatnonzero(v)[0]);c=int(v[k])
   if k in self.known:v=(v-c*self.known[k])%p
   else:
    v=v*pow(c,-1,p)%p
    assert not np.any(v@self.Bfree%p),'invalid known relation image'
    self.known[k]=v
    print('NEW valid cubic relation mod',p,'count',len(self.known),'needed',self.needed,flush=True)
    return
 def insert(self,z,v):
  p=self.p;v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.int64)@self.proj%p
  z={k:x%p for k,x in z.items() if x%p};self.rows+=1
  while z:
   k=min(z);c=z[k]
   if k not in self.unknown:
    a=pow(c,-1,p);z={j:x*a%p for j,x in z.items()};v=v*a%p
    self.unknown[k]=(z,v);return
   dz,dv=self.unknown[k]
   for j,x in dz.items():
    z[j]=(z.get(j,0)-c*x)%p
    if not z[j]:del z[j]
   v=(v-c*dv)%p
  self.known_insert(v)

def analyze(edgepaths,cache,ident,gs,gis):
 from agent2_rs_edge_preimage import edge_preimage_mod_gamma3
 H=Matrix(ZZ,json.load(open('workspace/agent1_class3_HNF215.json')))
 Hn=np.array(H.rows(),dtype=np.int64);A=H[:18,:36];piv=A.pivots()
 Ai=np.array(A[:,piv].inverse().rows(),dtype=np.int64)
 C=H[:,:36].transpose().right_kernel().basis_matrix()*H[:,36:]
 assert all(x%2==0 for x in C.list())
 C=row_basis(C.apply_map(lambda x:x//2))
 keys=[(i,w) for i in range(N) for w in __import__('itertools').product(range(N),repeat=4)]
 B=Matrix(ZZ,[[ld(w)[i].get(t,0) for w in L3] for i,t in keys])
 eliminators=[]
 for p in (2,3):
  E=Matrix(GF(p),C).echelon_form();r=E.rank();E=E[:r,:];pc=list(E.pivots());fc=[j for j in range(240) if j not in pc]
  P=np.zeros((240,len(fc)),dtype=np.int64)
  for j,k in enumerate(fc):P[k,j]=1
  for i,k in enumerate(pc):P[k,:]=-np.array([int(E[i,j]) for j in fc],dtype=np.int64)%p
  Bfree=np.array(B[:,fc].transpose().rows(),dtype=np.int64)%p
  eliminators.append(Eliminator(p,P,Bfree,197-r))
 unknowns={};polys={};wordlength={};started=time.time()
 def ep(f,fi):
  if f in polys:return polys[f]
  if f in cache:w=cache[f];key=None;sgn=0
  elif fi in cache:w=inv(cache[fi]);key=None;sgn=0
  else:
   key=min(f,fi);sgn=1 if f==key else -1
   if key not in unknowns:
    unknowns[key]=len(unknowns)
   base=edge_preimage_mod_gamma3(key)
   w=base if sgn==1 else inv(base)
   key=unknowns[key]
  out=(poly(w),len(w),key,sgn);polys[f]=out
  return out
 for idx,edges in enumerate(edgepaths):
  P=ZERO;z={};L=0
  for f,fi in edges:
   Q,n,k,s=ep(f,fi);L+=n;assert L<1000000,'int64 bound'
   P=pmul(P,Q)
   if k is not None:z[k]=z.get(k,0)+s
  if not z:continue # all exact-word loops already checked against H in prior certificates
  assert not np.any(P[0])
  r2=P[1];p3=2*P[2].copy()
  for i in range(M):
   for j in range(M):
    p3[i,i,j]+=r2[i,j];p3[i,j,j]+=r2[i,j]
  q2=np.array([r2[i,j] for i in range(M) for j in range(i+1,M)],dtype=np.int64)
  c=q2[list(piv)]@Ai
  assert np.array_equal(q2,c@Hn[:18,:36])
  d=coords({t:int(p3[t]) for t in __import__('itertools').product(range(M),repeat=3) if p3[t]})
  v=np.array(d,dtype=np.int64)-c@Hn[:18,36:]
  assert not np.any(v%2)
  v=v//2
  for el in eliminators:el.insert(z,v)
  if idx%500==0:print('RScentral loop',idx,'unknowns',len(unknowns),'newranks',[len(el.known) for el in eliminators],'seconds',round(time.time()-started,2),flush=True)
 print('RESULT loops',len(edgepaths),'unknowns',len(unknowns),'new relation ranks',[len(el.known) for el in eliminators],'needed',[el.needed for el in eliminators],flush=True)
 if all(len(el.known)==el.needed for el in eliminators):print('PASS integral degree3 IA3 Johnson injectivity')
 else:print('INCOMPLETE RScentral certificate')
 return eliminators
''')
# Produce a driver from our existing RS chunk builder, without exact completion.

"""Canonical IA3 edge lifts modulo gamma3, using certified c<=2 equality."""
import pickle
from pathlib import Path
from itertools import combinations,product
from sage.all import Matrix,vector,ZZ
from agent2_nielsen_actions import magnus
from degree2_certificate import compose,inv,red
from agent1_degree3 import expand,Ds,dbr

ident,gs,gis,names=magnus(3);pairs=list(combinations(range(9),2))
B=Matrix(ZZ,[[dbr(Ds[i],Ds[j])[k].get(w,0) for i,j in pairs] for k in range(3) for w in product(range(3),repeat=3)])
D,U,V=B.smith_form();rank=B.rank();assert rank==18
assert all(D[i,i]==1 for i in range(rank))
keys=[];sign=[]
for d in Ds:
 k=next(k for k,p in enumerate(d) if p);w=next(w for w in d[k] if w[0]<w[1]);keys.append((k,w));sign.append(d[k][w])

def evaluate(w):
 f=ident
 for x in w:f=compose(f,gs[x-1] if x>0 else gis[-x-1])
 return f

def powerword(w,n):return w*n if n>=0 else inv(w)*(-n)

def preimage(f):
 """Return a Magnus word w such that f*w^-1 is in A3=gamma3(IA3)."""
 f=tuple(map(tuple,f));ex=[expand(f[k]+(-k-1,)) for k in range(3)]
 assert not any(len(w)==1 for p in ex for w in p)
 aa=[ex[k].get(w,0)*s for (k,w),s in zip(keys,sign)]
 w1=tuple(x for i,a in enumerate(aa) for x in powerword((i+1,),a))
 # Residual r=f*w1^-1 is in A2=gamma2; compute its true tau2.
 r=compose(f,evaluate(inv(w1)));rx=[expand(r[k]+(-k-1,)) for k in range(3)]
 assert not any(len(w) in (1,2) for p in rx for w in p)
 y=vector(ZZ,[rx[k].get(w,0) for k in range(3) for w in product(range(3),repeat=3)])
 z=U*y
 assert all(x==0 for x in z[rank:])
 xx=V*vector(ZZ,list(z[:rank])+[0]*(36-rank));assert B*xx==y
 w2=()
 for (i,j),a in zip(pairs,xx):
  if a:w2+=powerword((i+1,j+1,-i-1,-j-1),int(a))
 w=red(w2+w1)
 return w

if __name__=='__main__':
 cache=pickle.loads(Path('workspace/agent2_rs_merged_edgecache.pkl').read_bytes())
 for f in list(cache)[:50]:
  w=preimage(f)
  r=compose(f,evaluate(inv(w)))
  assert all(not any(1<=len(t)<=3 for t in expand(r[k]+(-k-1,))) for k in range(3))
 print('PASS 50 actualSchreieredges matched moduloA3=gamma3')

function ( n )
    local F, F2, rels, sigma, tau, endos, itrels, a, b, c, d, t, r, u, v, T, U, V, G, e, f, g, h, i, IL;
    if n = 1 then
        Info( InfoLPRES, 1, "The Grigorchuk group on 4 generators from [Lys85]" );
        F := FreeGroup( "a", "b", "c", "d" );
        a := F.1;
        b := F.2;
        c := F.3;
        d := F.4;
        rels := [ a ^ 2, b ^ 2, c ^ 2, d ^ 2, b * c * d ];
        sigma := GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b, c, d ], [ c ^ a, d, b, c ] );
        endos := [ sigma ];
        itrels := [ Comm( d, d ^ a ), Comm( d, d ^ (a * c * a * c * a) ) ];
        G := LPresentedGroup( F, rels, endos, itrels );
        SetIsInvariantLPresentation( G, true );
        SetSize( G, infinity );
    elif n = 2 then
        Info( InfoLPRES, 1, "The Grigorchuk group on 3 generators" );
        F := FreeGroup( "a", "c", "d" );
        a := F.1;
        c := F.2;
        d := F.3;
        rels := [  ];
        sigma := GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, c, d ], [ c ^ a, c * d, c ] );
        endos := [ sigma ];
        itrels := [ a ^ 2, Comm( d, d ^ a ), Comm( d, d ^ (a * c * a * c * a) ) ];
        G := LPresentedGroup( F, rels, endos, itrels );
        SetSize( G, infinity );

        Info( InfoLPRES, 1, "The lamplighter group on two lamp states" );
        IL := InfoLevel( InfoLPRES );
        SetInfoLevel( InfoLPRES, 0 );

        SetInfoLevel( InfoLPRES, IL );
        SetSize( G, infinity );

        F := FreeGroup( "a", "b" );

v * U * T * V * u * v * U * t * v * u * T * u * V, u * V * t * V * U * T * v * U * V * u * t * v * U * v * T * U * v * t * u * V * u * T, u * V * t * V * U * T * V * U * t * U * V * T * u * V * u * t * v * U * v * T * u, u * V * t * V * U * T * v * t * U * v * U * T * V * u * V * u * t * v * U * v * T * U * t * v * u * T * u * V ];
        G := LPresentedGroup( F, rels, endos, itrels );
        SetSize( G, infinity );
    elif n = 9 then
        Info( InfoLPRES, 1, "The Basilica group" );
        F := FreeGroup( "a", "b" );
        a := F.1;
        b := F.2;
        rels := [  ];
        endos := [ GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b ], [ b ^ 2, a ] ) ];
        itrels := [ Comm( a, a ^ b ) ];
        G := LPresentedGroup( F, rels, endos, itrels );
        SetSize( G, infinity );
    elif n = 10 then
        Info( InfoLPRES, 1, "Baumslag's group" );
        F := FreeGroup( "a", "b", "t", "u" );
        a := F.1;
        b := F.2;
        t := F.3;
        u := F.4;
        rels := [ u / b ];
        endos := [ GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b, t, u ], [ a, b, t, u ^ t ] ), GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b, t, u ], [ a, b, t, u ^ (t ^ -1) ] ) ];
        itrels := [ a ^ t / a ^ 4, (b ^ 2) ^ t / b, Comm( a, u ) ];
        G := LPresentedGroup( F, rels, endos, itrels );
        SetIsInvariantLPresentation( G, false );
        SetSize( G, infinity );
    elif n = 11 then
        Info( InfoLPRES, 1, "The modified L-presentation of the Basilica Group" );
        F := FreeGroup( "a", "b" );
        a := F.1;
        b := F.2;
        rels := [  ];
        endos := [ GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b ], [ b ^ 2, a ] ), GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b ], [ a * b, a ^ 2 ] ) ];
        itrels := [ Comm( a, a ^ b ) ];
        G := LPresentedGroup( F, rels, endos, itrels );
        SetSize( G, infinity );
    elif n = 12 then
        Info( InfoLPRES, 1, "The Hanoi-Tower group from [BSZ09]" );
        F := FreeGroup( "a", "b", "c" );
        a := F.1;
        b := F.2;
        c := F.3;
        d := Comm( a, b );
        e := Comm( b, c );
        f := Comm( c, a );
        g := d ^ c;
        h := e ^ a;
        i := f ^ b;
        rels := [ a ^ 2, b ^ 2, c ^ 2 ];
        endos := [ GroupHomomorphismByImagesNC( F, F, [ a, b, c ], [ a, b ^ c, c ^ b ] ) ];
        itrels := [ d ^ -1 * e * f * i ^ -1 * g * e, h * e ^ -1 * d ^ -1 * f * d * i ^ -1, e ^ -1 * g ^ -1 * f ^ -1 * e * g * f, e ^ -1 * d * h * e ^ -2 * d ^ -1 * h ^ 2, h * g * d ^ -2 * f ^ -1 * g * f * e ^ -1 ];
        G := LPresentedGroup( F, rels, endos, itrels );
        SetIsInvariantLPresentation( G, true );
        SetSize( G, infinity );
    else
        Error( "<n> must be an integer less than 12" );
    fi;
    return G;
end

function ( d, n )
    local K, PK, R, S, D, name, ints, x;
    ints := [ -1, -2, -3, -5, -7, -11, -15 ];
    if not d in ints then
        Print( "GL(3,Sqrt(d)) is implemented for d=  -1, -2, -3, -5, -7, -11, -15  .\n" );
        return fail;
    fi;
    if IsInt( d ) then
        name := Concatenation( "GL(3,O", String( d ), "HAP)" );
    else
        name := d;
    fi;
    K := ContractibleGcomplex( name );
    R := FreeGResolution( K, n );
    if IsInt( d ) then
        name := name{[ 1 .. Length( name ) - 4 ]};
        name := Concatenation( name, ")" );
    fi;
    SetName( R!.group, name );
    return R;
end

function ( groupname )
    local C, G, StabilizerGroups, Stabilizer, lnth, dims, Dimension, Boundary, boundaryList, Elts, Rot, Stab, RotSubGroups, action, ActionRecord, TransMat, x, lstMathieu, lstAlexanderSebastianSL, lstSebastianGL, lstAlexanderSebastianAlt, n, k, s, BI, SGN, tmp, LstEl, bool, name, dd;
    n := Position( groupname, '-' );
    k := Position( groupname, ')' );
    if IsInt( n ) and IsInt( k ) then
        dd := groupname{[ n + 1 .. k - 1 ]};
    else
        dd := fail;
    fi;
    groupname := Filtered( groupname, function ( x )
            return not (x = '(' or x = ')' or x = ',' or x = '[' or x = ']');
        end );
    name := groupname;
    if name = "SL2Z" then
        return ContractibleSL2ZComplex(  );
    fi;
    groupname := Concatenation( "lib/Perturbations/Gcomplexes/", groupname );
    bool := ReadPackage( "HAP", groupname );
    if HAP_GCOMPLEX_SETUP[1] then
        TransMat := function ( x )
              return x ^ -1;
          end;
    else
        TransMat := function ( x )
              return x;
          end;
    fi;
    if bool = false then
        Print( "G-complexes are implemented for the following groups only:  \n\n" );
        lstMathieu := " SL(2,Z) , SL(3,Z) , PGL(3,Z[i]) , PGL(3,Eisenstein_Integers) ,  PSL(4,Z) , Sp(4,Z) , \n\n PSL(4,Z)_b , PSL(4,Z)_c , PSL(4,Z)_d \n";
        lstAlexanderSebastianAlt := " SL(2,O-d)_a , d=2, 7, 11, 19  \n";
        lstSebastianGL := " GL(2,O-d) , d=1, 2, 3, 5, 6, 7, 10, 11, 13, 14, 15, 17, 19, 21, 22, 23, 26, 43  \n";
        lstAlexanderSebastianSL := " SL(2,O-d) , d=2, 3, 5, 7, 10, 11, 13, 14, 15, 17, 19, 21, 22, 23, 26, 43, 67, 163 \n";
        Print( lstMathieu, "\n\n", lstSebastianGL, "\n\n", lstAlexanderSebastianSL, "\n\n", lstAlexanderSebastianAlt, "\n\nwhere subscripts _a, _b,  etc denote alternative complexes for a given group and  O-d denotes the ring of integers of the number field Q(sqrt(-d)).\n\n" );
        return fail;
    fi;
    C := StructuralCopy( HAP_GCOMPLEX_LIST );
    lnth := Length( C ) - 1;
    dims := List( [ 1 .. lnth + 1 ], function ( n )
            return Length( C[n] );
        end );
    Dimension := function ( n )
          if n > lnth then
              return 0;
          fi;
          return dims[n + 1];
      end;
    Elts := [ Identity( C[1][1].TheMatrixStab ) ];
    StabilizerGroups := [  ];
    RotSubGroups := [  ];
    boundaryList := [  ];
    for n in [ 1 .. lnth + 1 ] do
        boundaryList[n] := [  ];
        StabilizerGroups[n] := [  ];
        RotSubGroups[n] := [  ];
        for k in [ 1 .. Dimension( n - 1 ) ] do
            if IsFinite( C[n][k].TheMatrixStab ) then
                Append( Elts, Elements( C[n][k].TheMatrixStab ) );
            else
                Append( Elts, GeneratorsOfGroup( C[n][k].TheMatrixStab ) );
            fi;
            Add( StabilizerGroups[n], C[n][k].TheMatrixStab );
            Add( RotSubGroups[n], C[n][k].TheRotSubgroup );
        od;
    od;
    Elts := SSortedList( Elts );
    for n in [ 1 .. lnth + 1 ] do
        boundaryList[n] := [  ];
        for k in [ 1 .. Dimension( n - 1 ) ] do
            tmp := C[n][k].BoundaryImage;
            BI := tmp.ListIFace;
            SGN := tmp.ListSign;
            LstEl := List( tmp.ListElt, function ( w )
                    return TransMat( w );
                end );
            Append( Elts, Difference( LstEl, Elts ) );
            for s in [ 1 .. Length( BI ) ] do
                BI[s] := [ SGN[s] * BI[s], Position( Elts, LstEl[s] ) ];
            od;
            Add( boundaryList[n], BI );
        od;
    od;
    ActionRecord := [  ];
    for n in [ 1 .. lnth + 1 ] do
        ActionRecord[n] := [  ];
        for k in [ 1 .. Dimension( n - 1 ) ] do
            ActionRecord[n][k] := [  ];
        od;
    od;
    G := Group( One( Elts[1] ) * Elts );
    G!.bianchiInteger := dd;
    Boundary := function ( n, k )
          if k > 0 then
              return boundaryList[n + 1][k];
          else
              return NegateWord( boundaryList[n + 1][- k] );
          fi;
          return;
      end;
    Stabilizer := function ( n, k )
          return StabilizerGroups[n + 1][k];
      end;
    action := function ( n, k, g )
          local id, r, u, H, abk, ans;
          abk := AbsInt( k );
          if not IsBound( ActionRecord[n + 1][abk][g] ) then
              H := StabilizerGroups[n + 1][abk];
              if Order( H ) = infinity then
                  ActionRecord[n + 1][abk][g] := 1;
              else
                  id := CanonicalRightCosetElement( H, Identity( H ) );
                  r := CanonicalRightCosetElement( H, Elts[g] ^ -1 );
                  r := id ^ -1 * r;
                  u := r * Elts[g];
                  if u in RotSubGroups[n + 1][abk] then
                      ans := 1;
                  else
                      ans := -1;
                  fi;
                  ActionRecord[n + 1][abk][g] := ans;
              fi;
          fi;
          return ActionRecord[n + 1][abk][g];
      end;
    if name = "SL2Z" then
        G := SL( 2, Integers );
    fi;
    if name = "SL3Zs" or name = "SL3Za" then
        G := SL( 3, Integers );
    fi;
    if name = "SL4Z" or name = "SL4Z_b" or name = "SL4Z_c" or name = "SL4Z_d" then
        G := SL( 4, Integers );
    fi;
    SetName( G, "matrix group" );
    return Objectify( HapNonFreeResolution, rec(
          dimension := Dimension,
          boundary := Boundary,
          homotopy := fail,
          elts := Elts,
          group := G,
          stabilizer := Stabilizer,
          action := action,
          properties := [ [ "length", Maximum( 1000, lnth ) ], [ "characteristic", 0 ], [ "type", "resolution" ], [ "reduced", true ] ] ) );
end
function ( string, N )
    local C, R;
    C := ContractibleGcomplex( string );
    R := FreeGResolution( C, N );
    return R;
end

          return pos;

    FirstBoundaryHomomorphism := function ( x )
          local r;

          r := List( r, function ( y )

          if x[1] > 0 then

    Boundary2Relator := function ( b )
          local c, rel, w;

    COLOURS := [ "blue", "red", "green", "black", "brown", "yellow", "antiquewhite4", "aquamarine4", "bisque", "blueviolet" ];

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/agent1_hap_spherical_identities.py')
p.write_text('''"""Extract explicit free-group identities among SL3 relators from HAP3cells.
Every emitted identity is checked by literal free-word reduction.
"""
import json
from functools import lru_cache
from agent1_degree3 import red,inv

D=json.load(open('workspace/agent1_HAP_SL3Z_presentation_3cells_simple.json'))
E=[tuple(x for r in g for x in r) for g in D['matrix_elts']]
def mm(a,b):return tuple(sum(a[3*i+k]*b[3*k+j] for k in range(3)) for i in range(3) for j in range(3))
def endpoints(e,m):
 b=D['boundaries']['1'][e-1]
 n=next(E[i-1] for s,i in b if s<0);p=next(E[i-1] for s,i in b if s>0)
 return mm(m,n),mm(m,p)

def baseface(f):
 edges=[]
 for e,i in D['boundaries']['2'][f]:
  m=E[i-1];u,v=endpoints(abs(e),m)
  if e<0:u,v=v,u
  edges.append((u,v,e,(abs(e),m),1 if e>0 else -1))
 r=D['relators'][f]
 def dfs(out,unused):
  if not unused:return out if out[-1][1]==out[0][0] else None
  label=r[len(out)]
  for j in unused:
   ee=edges[j]
   if ee[0]==out[-1][1] and ee[2]==label:
    z=dfs(out+[ee],unused-{j})
    if z:return z
  return None
 for j,ee in enumerate(edges):
  if ee[2]==r[0]:
   z=dfs([ee],set(range(len(edges)))-{j})
   if z:return z
 raise ValueError(('no attaching polygon',f,r))

def cancel(poly):
 out=[]
 for ee in poly:
  if out and out[-1][3]==ee[3] and out[-1][4]==-ee[4]:out.pop()
  else:out.append(ee)
 # Do not cyclicallycancel acrossbasepoint; linearcancellationenough.
 return out

def labels(poly):return tuple(e[2] for e in poly)
def translate(poly,g,sgn):
 out=[]
 for u,v,e,(idx,m),s in poly:out.append((mm(g,u),mm(g,v),e,(idx,mm(g,m)),s))
 if sgn<0:out=[(v,u,-e,ID,-s) for u,v,e,ID,s in out[::-1]]
 return out

def termsword(terms):
 out=()
 for q,f,s in terms:
  r=tuple(D['relators'][f]);r=r if s>0 else inv(r)
  out=red(out+q+r+inv(q))
 return out

def main():
 faces=[baseface(f) for f in range(len(D['relators']))]
 allident=[]
 for cell,b3 in enumerate(D['boundaries']['3']):
  pol=[]
  for f,i in b3:
   sign=1 if f>0 else -1
   pol.append((translate(faces[abs(f)-1],E[i-1],sign),[((),abs(f)-1,sign)]))
  while len(pol)>1:
   found=None
   for ia in range(len(pol)):
    for ib in range(ia+1,len(pol)):
     A,ta=pol[ia];B,tb=pol[ib]
     for a,ea in enumerate(A):
      for b,eb in enumerate(B):
       if ea[3]==eb[3] and ea[4]==-eb[4]:found=(ia,ib,a,b);break
      if found:break
     if found:break
    if found:break
   if found is None:break
   ia,ib,a,b=found;A,ta=pol[ia];B,tb=pol[ib]
   S=labels(A[:a+1]);Q=labels(B[:b]);conj=red(S+inv(Q))
   Brot=B[b:]+B[:b];new=cancel(A[:a]+Brot[1:]+A[a+1:])
   assert red(conj+labels(B)+inv(conj)+labels(A))==red(labels(new))
   terms=[(red(conj+q),f,s) for q,f,s in tb]+ta
   assert termsword(terms)==red(labels(new))
   for i in sorted((ia,ib),reverse=True):pol.pop(i)
   pol.append((new,terms))
  if len(pol)==1 and not red(labels(pol[0][0])):
   terms=pol[0][1];assert not termsword(terms)
   allident.append({'cell':cell+1,'terms':terms})
   print('VERIFIED FREE IDENTITY cell',cell+1,'terms',len(terms),flush=True)
  else:print('cell',cell+1,'not reduced toidentity; remainingpolygons',len(pol),'lengths',[len(p) for p,t in pol],flush=True)
 json.dump(allident,open('workspace/agent1_HAP_SL3Z_free_identities.json','w'))
 return allident
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/agent1_hap_identity_c3.py')
p.write_text('''import json
from functools import lru_cache
from agent1_degree3 import *
from agent1_degree3_closure import initial_vector
from agent1_peiffer_c3 import ew,ec,ei,ID,ONE,subst,tmul

def main():
 acts=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))['actions']
 order=['T','P','I','C'];AA=[ew(acts[x]['words']) for x in order]
 AI=[ei(a) for a in AA]
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_hap_relator_words.json'))
 ids=json.load(open('workspace/agent1_HAP_SL3Z_free_identities.json'))
 def actword(w,gens,invs):
  e=ID
  for s in w:e=ec(e,gens[s-1] if s>0 else invs[-s-1])
  return e
 GG=[actword(d['word'],AA,AI) for d in data['lifts']];GI=[ei(a) for a in GG]
 @lru_cache(None)
 def aq(q):return actword(q,GG,GI)
 rows=[]
 for identity in ids:
  out=ONE
  fw=()
  for q,f,s in identity['terms']:
   r=tuple(data['relators'][f]);fw=red(fw+tuple(q)+(r if s>0 else inv(r))+inv(tuple(q)))
   w=data['magnus_relator_lifts'][f]
   assert w is not None
   p=expand(tuple(w) if s>0 else inv(tuple(w)))
   out=tmul(out,add(subst(add(p,ONE,-1),aq(tuple(q))),ONE))
  assert not fw,'source identity notfree'
  z=initial_vector(out);rows.append(z)
  print('translated genuine3cell IArelator',identity['cell'],'nonzeroentries',sum(x!=0 for x in z),flush=True)
 json.dump(rows,open('workspace/agent1_hap_identity_c3_vectors.json','w'))
 return rows
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')

from collections import defaultdict
from agent1_hap_spherical_identities import *

def trial(cell,rng):
 ps=[]
 for f,i in D['boundaries']['3'][cell]:
  s=1 if f>0 else -1
  ps.append((translate(baseface(abs(f)-1),E[i-1],s),[((),abs(f)-1,s)]))
 found=[]
 while len(ps)>1:
  pool=defaultdict(list)
  for j,(P,T) in enumerate(ps):
   for k,ee in enumerate(P):pool[ee[3]].append((j,k,ee[4]))
  choices=[]
  for vv in pool.values():
   for a in vv:
    for b in vv:
     if a[0]<b[0] and a[2]==-b[2]:choices.append((a[0],b[0],a[1],b[1]))
  if not choices:break
  ia,ib,a,b=rng.choice(choices);A,ta=ps[ia];B,tb=ps[ib]
  conj=red(labels(A[:a+1])+inv(labels(B[:b])))
  new=cancel(A[:a]+(B[b:]+B[:b])[1:]+A[a+1:])
  terms=[(red(conj+q),f,s) for q,f,s in tb]+ta
  for i in sorted((ia,ib),reverse=True):ps.pop(i)
  if not red(labels(new)):
   assert not termsword(terms)
   found.append(terms)
  else:ps.append((new,terms))
 for P,T in ps:
  if not red(labels(P)):
   assert not termsword(T);found.append(T)
 return found

def main(count=100):
 rng=random.Random(104257);out=[];seen=set();start=time.time()
 for cell in range(7,12):
  got=0
  for j in range(count):
   for terms in trial(cell,rng):
    key=tuple((q,f,s) for q,f,s in terms)
    if key in seen:continue
    seen.add(key);out.append({'cell':cell+1,'terms':terms});got+=1
  print('random cell',cell+1,'identities',got,'time',round(time.time()-start,2),flush=True)
 json.dump(out,open('workspace/agent1_HAP_SL3Z_random_free_identities.json','w'))
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':main(30)
''')

p.write_text('''"""Fast exact12log coordinates and linearAut action through class3."""
import json,sys
from functools import lru_cache
from itertools import combinations
from agent1_degree3 import *
from agent1_degree3_closure import coords2,L2,linear_sub
from agent1_peiffer_c3 import ew,ec,ei,ID,ONE,subst,tmul

pairs=list(combinations(range(m),2))
def scale(p,k):return {w:k*x for w,x in p.items() if k*x}
def div(p,k):
 assert all(x%k==0 for x in p.values())
 return {w:x//k for w,x in p.items() if x}
def logs12(p):
 a={w:x for w,x in p.items() if len(w)==1}
 b={w:x for w,x in p.items() if len(w)==2}
 c={w:x for w,x in p.items() if len(w)==3}
 diag={(i,i):x for (i,),x in a.items()}
 b12=add(add(scale(b,12),scale(diag,6)),scale(mul(a,a,3),6),-1)
 c12=scale(c,12)
 for (i,j),x in b.items():c12=add(c12,{(i,i,j):6*x,(i,j,j):6*x})
 for (i,),x in a.items():c12=add(c12,{(i,i,i):2*x})
 c12=add(c12,scale(add(mul(a,b,3),mul(b,a,3)),6),-1)
 c12=add(c12,scale(add(mul(a,diag,3),mul(diag,a,3)),3),-1)
 c12=add(c12,scale(mul(mul(a,a,3),a,3),4))
 return [12*a.get((i,),0) for i in range(m)]+coords2(b12)+coords(c12)

def polys(v):
 a={(i,):v[i] for i in range(m) if v[i]};b={};c={}
 for x,p in zip(v[m:m+36],L2):b=add(b,p,x)
 for x,p in zip(v[m+36:],LP):c=add(c,p,x)
 return a,b,c

def bch12(X,Y):
 a,b,c=X;d,e,f=Y
 z1=add(a,d);z2=add(add(b,e),div(br(a,d),24))
 z3=add(add(c,f),div(add(br(a,e),br(b,d)),24))
 z3=add(z3,div(add(br(a,br(a,d)),br(d,br(d,a))),1728))
 return z1,z2,z3

def amatrix(E):
 vv=[logs12(add(p,ONE)) for p in E]
 A=[{(j,):v[j]//12 for j in range(m) if v[j]} for v in vv]
 Q=[]
 for v in vv:Q.append(div(polys(v)[1],6))
 cols=[v for v in vv]
 for i,j in pairs:
  b=scale(br(A[i],A[j]),12)
  c=scale(add(br(Q[i],A[j]),br(A[i],Q[j])),6)
  cols.append([0]*m+coords2(b)+coords(c))
 for p in LP:cols.append([0]*(m+36)+[12*x for x in coords(linear_sub(p,A))])
 rows=[]
 for i in range(285):rows.append([(j,cols[j][i]) for j in range(285) if cols[j][i]])
 return rows

def applymat(M,v):
 out=[]
 for row in M:
  x=sum(a*v[j] for j,a in row);assert x%12==0;out.append(x//12)
 return tuple(out)

def main(identity_file='workspace/agent1_HAP_SL3Z_random_free_identities.json',out_file='workspace/agent1_hap_fast_c3_vectors.json'):
 sys.setrecursionlimit(50000)
 acts=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))['actions']
 AA=[ew(acts[x]['words']) for x in ['T','P','I','C']];AI=[ei(a) for a in AA]
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_hap_relator_words_ALL.json'))
 def aw(w,G,GI):
  e=ID
  for s in w:e=ec(e,G[s-1] if s>0 else GI[-s-1])
  return e
 GG=[aw(d['word'],AA,AI) for d in data['lifts']];GI=[ei(a) for a in GG]
 matrices={i+1:amatrix(g) for i,g in enumerate(GG)}
 matrices.update({-i-1:amatrix(g) for i,g in enumerate(GI)})
 print('built6linear285dimAutmatrices',flush=True)
 R=[tuple(logs12(expand(tuple(w)))) for w in data['magnus_relator_lifts']]
 @lru_cache(None)
 def act(q,f):
  if not q:return R[f]
  return applymat(matrices[q[0]],act(q[1:],f))
 rows=[]
 for ident in json.load(open(identity_file)):
  X=({}, {}, {});fw=()
  for q,f,s in ident['terms']:
   q=tuple(q);r=tuple(data['relators'][f]);fw=red(fw+q+(r if s>0 else inv(r))+inv(q))
   v=act(q,f)
   if s<0:v=tuple(-x for x in v)
   X=bch12(X,polys(v))
  assert not fw
  assert not X[0]
  z=coords2(div(X[1],12))+coords(div(X[2],6))
  rows.append(z)
  json.dump(rows,open(out_file,'w'))
  print('translated verifiedcell',ident['cell'],'nterms',len(ident['terms']),'nonzeroentries',sum(x!=0 for x in z),'actioncache',act.cache_info(),flush=True)
 return rows
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')

"""Exact integral nilpotent quotients of the certified central IA3 cover.
This is NOT a presentation of IA3; central source artifacts may remain.
"""
import json,time,sys
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter
from sage.all import *
from sage.libs.gap.libgap import libgap

def build():
 assert bool(libgap.eval('LoadPackage("lpres",false:OnlyNeeded:=true)'))
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_compact_cover_seeds.json'))
 F=libgap.FreeGroup(9);gen=list(libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(F))
 def word(w):
  q=libgap.One(F)
  for x in w:q=q*gen[abs(x)-1]**(1 if x>0 else -1)
  return q
 endos=[libgap.GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,F,gen,[word(w) for w in a]) for a in d['endomorphisms']]
 rels=[word(w) for w in d['iterated_relators'] if w]
 H=libgap.LPresentedGroup(F,[],endos,rels)
 return H

def run(c=3):
 H=build();t=time.time();Q=libgap.NilpotentQuotient(H,c)
 print('class',c,'integralNQtime',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 lcs=list(libgap.LowerCentralSeries(Q));invariants=[]
 for i in range(len(lcs)-1):
  inv=[int(x) for x in libgap.AbelianInvariants(libgap.FactorGroup(lcs[i],lcs[i+1]))]
  invariants.append(inv);print('LCSfactor',i+1,'counts',dict(Counter(inv)),flush=True)
 Path('workspace/agent2_cover_class%d_invariants.json'%c).write_text(json.dumps(invariants))
 return H,Q,invariants
if __name__=='__main__':run(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 3)

                             The FR package

This is the README file for the GAP package "FR".

This package implements Functionally Recursive and Mealy automata in
GAP. These objects can be manipulated as group elements, and various
specific commands allow their manipulation as automorphisms of infinite
rooted trees. Permutation quotients can also be created and manipulated
as standard GAP groups or semigroups.

This release (1.0) is the first containing all the planned functionality,
though some rough edges will still need to be smoothed. In particular,
the TODO file describes plans for future development, including
computations of presentations for self-similar groups, interval ari

from functools import lru_cache
from agent1_fast_c3_action import *

def main():
 sys.setrecursionlimit(50000)
 acts=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))['actions']
 names=['T','P','I','C'];AA=[ew(acts[x]['words']) for x in names];AI=[ei(a) for a in AA]
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_hap_relator_words_ALL.json'))
 def aw(w,G,GI):
  e=ID
  for s in w:e=ec(e,G[s-1] if s>0 else GI[-s-1])
  return e
 GG=[aw(d['word'],AA,AI) for d in data['lifts']]
 CC=AA[3];CCI=AI[3];II=AA[2];I1=ec(CC,ec(II,CCI));I2=ec(CC,ec(I1,CCI))
 GG.append(ec(II,ec(I1,I2)));GI=[ei(a) for a in GG]
 MM={i+1:amatrix(g) for i,g in enumerate(GG)};MM.update({-i-1:amatrix(g) for i,g in enumerate(GI)})
 prod=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_GL3_product_identities.json'))
 diffs={d['name']:d for d in json.load(open('workspace/agent3_HAP_comparison_differences_EXACT.json'))}
 vals=[tuple(logs12(expand(tuple(w)))) for w in prod['relator_magnus_words']]
 vals +=[tuple(logs12(expand(tuple(diffs[n]['magnus_word'])))) for n in names]
 L=[((4,) if diffs[n]['epsilon'] else ())+tuple(diffs[n]['q']) for n in names]
 @lru_cache(None)
 def act(q,f):
  if not q:return vals[f]
  return applymat(MM[q[0]],act(q[1:],f))
 rows=[]
 fill=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_hap_comparison_fillings.json'))
 for key,rec in fill['fillings'].items():
  X=({}, {}, {});prefix=()
  for s in rec['aut_operator_relator']:
   i=abs(s)-1
   if s>0:
    v=act(prefix,11+i);X=bch12(X,polys(v));prefix=red(prefix+L[i])
   else:
    prefix=red(prefix+inv(L[i]));v=act(prefix,11+i);X=bch12(X,polys(tuple(-x for x in v)))
  assert prefix==tuple(rec['hap_GL_word'])
  fw=()
  for idx,u in rec['terms_u_inverse_r_u']:
   j=abs(idx)-1;r=tuple(prod['relators'][j]);u=tuple(u)
   fw=red(fw+inv(u)+(r if idx>0 else inv(r))+u)
   v=act(inv(u),j)
   if idx<0:v=tuple(-x for x in v)
   X=bch12(X,polys(v))
  assert fw==prefix

  z=coords2(div(X[1],12))+coords(div(X[2],6));rows.append(z)
  print('VALUEcomparison relator',key,'nterms',len(rec['terms_u_inverse_r_u']),'nonzeros',sum(x!=0 for x in z),flush=True)
 json.dump(rows,open('workspace/agent1_hap_value_c3_vectors.json','w'))
 return rows
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')
