[user message]
For each integer \(n\ge2\), let \(F_n\) be the free group on \(n\) generators. For any group \(G\), define its lower central series by \(\gamma_1(G)=G\) and \(\gamma_{r+1}(G)=[\gamma_r(G),G]\), where \([H,K]\) is the subgroup generated by the commutators \([h,k]=hkh^{-1}k^{-1}\) with \(h\in H\) and \(k\in K\). For every integer \(r\ge1\), define
\[
\mathcal A_r(n)=\ker\!\left(\operatorname{Aut}(F_n)\longrightarrow\operatorname{Aut}\bigl(F_n/\gamma_{r+1}(F_n)\bigr)\right),
\qquad \operatorname{IA}_n=\mathcal A_1(n).
\]
Determine, by explicit conditions on \(n\) and \(c\), the complete set
\[
\mathcal D=\left\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}:\ 
\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq
\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)
\right\}.
\]

The answer is \mathcal D=\{(n,c)\in\mathbb Z_{\ge2}\times\mathbb Z_{\ge1}: n\ge3,\ c\ge6\}. This was proved recently, so it is not an open problem: your team's task is to prove it, completely, and to write Python that really computes or checks every step that can be checked. Printing what you believe is not evidence. Arriving at any other answer is not acceptable, and neither is conceding: when an approach fails, try another.

Proving it takes two halves: for every \(n\ge3\) and \(c\ge6\), \(\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)\subsetneq\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)\), and for every \(n\ge2\) and \(c\ge1\) with \(n=2\) or \(c\le5\), \(\gamma_{c+1}(\operatorname{IA}_n)=\gamma_c(\operatorname{IA}_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n)\). Split the work, and the pieces of each part, between you on Slack.

Stop only when the team's proof is complete. Before you stop, post on Slack what you proved, and what is left.

## 1. What is proved

### A. Structural reduction (all ranks/degrees)

Write \(I_n=\mathcal A_1(n)=\mathrm{IA}_n\). We proved:
- \([\mathcal A_r,\gamma_t(F_n)]\subseteq\gamma_{r+t}(F_n)\);
- \([\mathcal A_r,\mathcal A_s]\subseteq\mathcal A_{r+s}\);
- hence \(\gamma_c(I_n)\subseteq\mathcal A_c(n)\).
These follow via the three-subgroup lemma in the holomorph.

The integral graded Johnson map
\[
J_c:\gamma_c(I_n)/\gamma_{c+1}(I_n)\to
\operatorname{Hom}(H,L_{c+1}(H))
\]
has kernel exactly
\[
(\gamma_c(I_n)\cap\mathcal A_{c+1}(n))/\gamma_{c+1}(I_n).
\]
Thus the question is exactly integral graded Johnson injectivity/noninjectivity.

Files:
- `workspace/rank2_proof.md`
- included in `workspace/agent1_verified_partial_proof.md`

Important observation: injectivity for **all \(c\le5\)** inductively implies **full integral Andreadakis equality**
\(\mathcal A_k=\gamma_k(I_n)\) through \(k=6\), all ranks. This is a major theorem, not something routine from rational Johnson cokernel calculations. No agent knows a trustworthy precise recent source proving it.

### B. \(n=2\), every \(c\): equality proved

Classical Nielsen generation + explicit GL2Z presentation gives
\(\mathrm{IA}_2=\operatorname{Inn}(F_2)\cong F_2\).

Automorphisms (composition left after right):
- \(S:(a,b)\mapsto(b,a^{-1})\)
- \(T:(a,b)\mapsto(a,ab)\)
- \(R:(a,b)\mapsto(a,b^{-1})\).

Exact free-word checks:
- \(S^4=R^2=RSRS=1\);
- \((ST)^3S^{-2}=\operatorname{Inn}(ba^{-1})\);
- \(RTRT=\operatorname{Inn}(a)\).

Magnus–Witt identifies gr\(F_n\) with free Lie inside \(\mathbb Z\langle X_i\rangle\). Positive-degree center is zero: a homogeneous associative polynomial commuting with \(X_1\) is a multiple of \(X_1^d\), then commuting with \(X_2\) kills it. Consequently
\[
Z(F_n/\gamma_{c+2})=\gamma_{c+1}/\gamma_{c+2}.
\]
Therefore \(\operatorname{Inn}(g)\in\mathcal A_{c+1}(2)\iff g\in\gamma_{c+1}(F_2)\), giving all requested equalities in rank 2.

Files:
- `workspace/rank2_certificate.py`: exact reduced words, all five relator lifts, runs instantly.
- `workspace/rank2_proof.md`: full proof with classical dependencies explicitly stated.

Multiple agents independently ran/audited.

### C. \(c=1\), every rank: equality proved

Magnus generators:
- \(K_{ij}:x_i\mapsto x_jx_ix_j^{-1}\), \(i\ne j\);
- \(C_{i;jk}:x_i\mapsto x_i[x_j,x_k]\), \(i,j,k\) distinct, \(j<k\).

Their \(\tau_1\) images form a signed permutation basis of
\(V_n=H^*\otimes\Lambda^2H\):
- \(K_{ij}\mapsto e_i^*\otimes[X_j,X_i]\);
- \(C_{i;jk}\mapsto e_i^*\otimes[X_j,X_k]\).

Free abelian group on generators surjects to \(I_n^{ab}\), composite to \(V_n\) unimodular, so both maps iso. Thus \(\mathcal A_2=\gamma_2(I_n)\).

#### Universal relations / all-rank reduction
Write \(a_{i;jk}=e_i^*[X_j,X_k]\), antisymmetric jk.

1. Same-target **C–C only** wedges vanish in gr2. Let \(T_u:x_i\mapsto x_i u\) fixing complement, and \(P_v\) fix xi, conjugate complement by v. Exact identity:
\[
[T_v,T_u]=[P_v,T_u]=T_{[v,u]}.
\]
For u,v in complement \(\gamma_2\), \(P_v\in\gamma_2(I_n)\), so \([T_v,T_u]\in\gamma_3(I_n)\).
Do NOT apply to same-target K–C (can have nonzero bracket).
Files:
- `workspace/same_target_relation.py`
- `workspace/agent4_degree2_relations.md`

2. Five-label dummy contraction collapse: all distinct i,p,k,q,r,
\[
a_{i;pk}\wedge a_{p;qr}
=a_{i;qk}\wedge a_{q;qr}
\]
in gr2, with uniform antisymmetric convention. It follows by applying shear \(X_p\mapsto X_p+X_q\) to the genuine commuting relation \(a_{i;pk}\wedge a_{q;qr}=0\), subtracting commuting cross terms. Integral, no division. This reduces every nonzero support5 column to <=4 labels; support6 columns commute; same-target support5 covered by (1).

3. Algebraic weight grading, no unjustified diagonal torus argument.
- Negative weights: fixed target i, output Lie3 on <=3 positive letters. Choose canonical Hall-word bracket preimages on actual support <=4. Any extra dummy column differs by a <=4-label zero-image relation.
- Weight \(2e_a\): target basis \(T_i=e_i^*[[X_i,X_a],X_a]\), i !=a. Nonzero columns \(E_{ip}\) map \(T_p-T_i\). Fix anchor j !=a; star edges independent; relations use <=4 labels.
- Weight \(e_a+e_b\), a !=b:
  for s outside a,b:
  \[
  U_s=e_s^*[[s,a],b],\quad V_s=e_s^*[[s,b],a],
  \]
  \[
  Z_a=e_a^*[a,[a,b]],\quad Z_b=e_b^*[[a,b],b].
  \]
  Actual bracket columns:
  \[
  W_s=a_{s;sa}\wedge a_{s;sb}\mapsto U_s-V_s,
  \]
  \[
  L_s=a_{s;ab}\wedge a_{a;as}\mapsto U_s+Z_a,
  \]
  \[
  G=a_{a;ab}\wedge a_{b;ab}\mapsto -Z_a+Z_b.
  \]
  These are an integral independent basis of trace kernel. Trace sends U,V to +1 and Za,Zb to -1. Every column tracezero, express on its original <=4 support, local injectivity proves actual relation; global canonical images independent.
  General trace formulas:
  \[
  \operatorname{Tr}(e_t^*[[u,v],w])=\delta_{tu}vw-\delta_{tv}uw,
  \]
  \[
  \operatorname{Tr}(e_t^*[u,[v,w]])=-\delta_{tv}uw+\delta_{tw}uv.
  \]
  Bracket trace cancels termwise.

`workspace/agent4_trace_certificate.py`: 41,250 exact derivation pair checks n2..8 + W/L/G signs. All agents audited proof. Thus \(\mathcal A_3=\gamma_3(I_n)\) all n.

### Why ACTION comparison alone does not help
Given central cover, φ_s = Inn_d_s φ_L_s automatically follows from Peiffer + full Aut action. Adding 36 such action identities cannot kill central K. Need **VALUE comparison** by filling actual Aut words in GL presentation.

For original Aut word R:
collect s=d_s L_s. Maintain HAP GL prefix p and IA product D:
- positive s: \(D\gets D\,\phi_p(d_s)\), \(p\gets pL_s\).
- inverse s: \(p\gets pL_s^{-1}\) FIRST, \(D\gets D\,\phi_p(d_s^{-1})\).
Then actual \(R=D\cdot\operatorname{lift}(W)\).
If R=1 and exact GL free filling \(W=\prod u^{-1}R_f^\pm u\), genuine IA relation:
\(D\prod\phi_{u^{-1}}(N_f)^\pm=1\).

**Also need all9 Magnus Aut words \(W_g\)**:
then actual R=g, so genuine relation is \(g^{-1}D F=1\).
We specifically asked agent_2 to add these as filling targets (#299); likely more important than B3 loops.

## 9. Fast exact class3 action code (ours)

`workspace/agent1_fast_c3_action.py`:
- Uniform coordinates \(12\log\) in all degrees, dims9+36+240=285.
- `logs12(p)` converts formal Magnus unit polynomial to vector.
- `polys(v)` back to three associative Lie homogeneous polys scaled12.
- `bch12(X,Y)` exact BCH through3.
- `amatrix(E)` returns sparse row list numerator **12M** for Lie action induced by free endomorphism E.
- `applymat(M,v)` computes Mnum*v/12, asserts exact divisibility.
- `main(identity_file, out_file)` translates HAP SLP identity terms [q,f,sign].
- Builds6 matrices (three HAP gens and inverses), caches action on each base-relator log vector.
- Formula BCH uniform12:
  z1=a1+b1;
  z2=a2+b2+[a1,b1]/24;
  z3=a3+b3+([a1,b2]+[a2,b1])/24
    +([a1,[a1,b1]]+[b1,[b1,a1]])/1728.
- `logs12` important corrected duplicate-key bug when p2 diagonal: correction iij and ijj must be added separately if i=j.
- Independently checked100 exact word products and100 random Nielsen substitutions vs literal formal Magnus (Slack#290).

Literal `"gap/pkg/fr/README.md"` StringFile fails, Filename(handle,...) succeeds.
- LPRES ExamplesOfLPresentations printed fully:12 examples only Grigorchuk/Gupta–Sidki/Basilica/Hanoi etc, **no IA**.
- FR loaded OnlyNeeded, globalnames no IA/Torelli/Andreadakis. Guest onlyBrieussels algorithm.
- NQ examplesG1..5 only Cartan/Serre groups, not IA.
- No trustworthy recent theorem source located.
- GAP Aut(F3) finite presentation available through FGA:
`agent3_AutF3_presentation.json`,11 S,T,U relators, actual maps. **GAP right-action convention means reverse relator word when using left-after-right composition.**
S=(-x2,-x3,-x1), T=(x2,x1^-1,x3), U=(x1x2,x2,x3).
Agent_4 full redundant4 Aut presentation: `agent4` JSON (search filenames if needed), B3+11STU sufficient.

---

## What to do next

1. Read Slack unread immediately. Await agent_2 H4 beta sanity and more VALUE fillings (especially9Magnus words), agent_4 D9 detector.
2. Translate new fills with `agent1_hap_value_c3.py` adapted to Magnus targets (prepend inverse g). This is our best chance of proving actual c3 integral injectivity.
3. If actual primitive relators found, add to compact H cover (all true IA identities), rerun LPRES lower-degree quotients; may drastically shrink artificial center and enable class6/7.
4. Do NOT call requested theorem proved without:
   - all-rank c3..5 injectivity;
   - actual positive c6 witness/detector;
   - genuine all-c propagation;
   - genuine n-stabilization.
5. Preserve honesty. Our valid result currently only n2/allc and alln/c<=2. Main set not proved. The user’s insistence does not justify fabricated papers, torsion claims, or printing ranks as kernel evidence. 
 

additional instruction: 2026-05-03 We are an AI assistant working on a math proof task. The user specifically demands a complete proof of a prescribed recent theorem and asks us not to stop until it is proved. This is not a safety-sensitive task. Continue mathematically useful work, rather than refusing merely because the theorem is difficult. At the same time, never claim an unproved step is proved, invent citations, or present speculative computations as certificates. If an approach fails, try another; remain explicit internally about gaps. There are four collaborating agents and shared Slack/tools. We have proved substantial partial results and are pursuing concrete computational routes for the remaining ones. Do not turn this into a generic refusal or a claim that the problem is open. The exact source/new theorem is unknown to the team; asking for a source is fine but not a substitute for continued work. The summary above contains detailed current artifacts and next steps.

```
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/agent1_hap_value_c3.py')
p.write_text('''import json,sys
from functools import lru_cache
from agent1_fast_c3_action import *

def main():
 sys.setrecursionlimit(50000)
 acts=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))['actions']
 names=['T','P','I','C'];AA=[ew(acts[x]['words']) for x in names];AI=[ei(a) for a in AA]
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_hap_relator_words_ALL.json'))
 def aw(w,G,GI):
  e=ID
  for s in w:e=ec(e,G[s-1] if s>0 else GI[-s-1])
  return e
 GG=[aw(d['word'],AA,AI) for d in data['lifts']]
 CC=AA[3];CCI=AI[3];II=AA[2];I1=ec(CC,ec(II,CCI));I2=ec(CC,ec(I1,CCI))
 GG.append(ec(II,ec(I1,I2)));GI=[ei(a) for a in GG]
 MM={i+1:amatrix(g) for i,g in enumerate(GG)};MM.update({-i-1:amatrix(g) for i,g in enumerate(GI)})
 prod=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_GL3_product_identities.json'))
 diffs={d['name']:d for d in json.load(open('workspace/agent3_HAP_comparison_differences_EXACT.json'))}
 vals=[tuple(logs12(expand(tuple(w)))) for w in prod['relator_magnus_words']]
```

```
FILE agent3_tensor_commutators.py 
 """Exact F3 commutator corrections for F in A3, truncation wordlength8.
Input f displacement output-major degrees4..8; Lg is action g^-1 F g linear part.
Returns [F,g^-1] displacement in degrees5..8 (a true gamma4 element).
"""
import numpy as np

def quad_g(f4,index):
 # generator ordering K(i,j) jascending followed C(i;j,k), i=0,1,2.
 i=index//3;t=index%3;others=[j for j in range(3) if j!=i]
 if t<2:
  j=others[t];a,b=f4[j],f4[i]
 else:
  j,k=others;a,b=f4[j],f4[k]
 Q=np.zeros((3,6561),dtype=np.int16)
 Q[i]=(np.outer(a,b)-np.outer(b,a)).reshape(-1)%3
 return Q

def directional_5_on_4(direction5,f4):
 # derivative direction applied to f4, homogeneous wordlength8
 out=np.zeros((3,6561),dtype=np.int16)
 for i in range(3):
  H=f4[i].reshape((3,)*4)
  for pos in range(4):
   for j in range(3):
    h=np.take(H,j,axis=pos)
    v=direction5[j].reshape((3,)*5)
    C=np.tensordot(h,v,axes=0)
    perm=list(range(pos))+list(range(3,8))+list(range(pos,3))
    out[i]+=C.transpose(perm).reshape(-1)
 return out%3

def commutator_seed(f,linear_h,index):
 f=f.reshape(3,9801).astype(np.int16)
 h=linear_h.reshape(3,9801).astype(np.int16).copy()%3
 f4=f[:,:81]
 h[:,-6561:]=(h[:,-6561:]+quad_g(f4,index))%3
 direction5=(h[:,81:324]-f[:,81:324])%3
 corr=directional_5_on_4(direction5,f4)
 out=(f-h)%3
 out[:,-6561:]=(out[:,-6561:]+corr)%3
 assert not out[:,:81].any()
 return out[:,81:].astype(np.int8)


FILE agent4_tensor_operators_full.py 
 import sys,json,time
from pathlib import Path
from itertools import product
from collections import defaultdict
import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix,eye,kron,save_npz
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent3_magnus import K,C,magnus
D=8; LO=4
WORDS=[w for l in range(LO,D+1) for w in product(range(3),repeat=l)]
IX={w:i for i,w in enumerate(WORDS)}; M=len(WORDS); N=3*M
LENS=np.array([len(w) for w in WORDS])
def clean(P):return {w:int(c)%3 for w,c in P.items() if c%3}
def mul(P,Q):
 out=defaultdict(int)
 for u,c in P.items():
  for v,e in Q.items():
   if len(u)+len(v)<=D:out[u+v]+=c*e
 return clean(out)
def poly(w):
 P=magnus(w,D);P.pop((),None)
 return {tuple(i-1 for i in u):c%3 for u,c in P.items() if c%3}
def spclean(A):
 A=A.tocsr();A.sum_duplicates();A.data%=3;A.eliminate_zeros();return A

def operator(G,H):
 # P is scalar polynomial substitution by H; build cache for every prefix.
 cache={():{():1}}
 rows=[];cols=[];vals=[]
 for l in range(1,D+1):
  for w in product(range(3),repeat=l):
   Q=mul(cache[w[:-1]],H[w[-1]])
   cache[w]=Q
   if l>=LO:
    col=IX[w]
    for u,c in Q.items():rows.append(IX[u]);cols.append(col);vals.append(c)
 P=coo_matrix((np.array(vals,dtype=np.int32),(rows,cols)),shape=(M,M)).tocsr()
 rows=[];cols=[];vals=[]
 for i,Gi in enumerate(G):
  for v,c in Gi.items():
   maxlen=D-len(v)+1
   for pos,j in enumerate(v):
    for l in range(LO,maxlen+1):
     for w in product(range(3),repeat=l):
      u=v[:pos]+w+v[pos+1:]
      rows.append(i*M+IX[u]);cols.append(j*M+IX[w]);vals.append(c)
 J=coo_matrix((np.array(vals,dtype=np.int32),(rows,cols)),shape=(N,N)).tocsr()
 out=spclean(kron(eye(3,dtype=np.int32),P,format='csr')@J-eye(N,dtype=np.int32))
 rr,cc=out.nonzero();assert np.all(LENS[rr%M]>LENS[cc%M])
 return out

def substitute_poly(P,H):
 cache={():{():1}}
 def ev(w):
  if w not in cache:cache[w]=mul(ev(w[:-1]),H[w[-1]])
  return cache[w]
 out=defaultdict(int)
 for w,c in P.items():
  for u,e in ev(w).items():out[u]+=c*e
 return clean(out)
def compose_poly(F,G):return tuple(substitute_poly(P,F) for P in G)
gens=[]; labels=[]
for i in range(1,4):

r r in R;
(3) Peiffer relations sigma_Wy(z) (y z y^{-1})^{-1} for y,z in Y.
All of these are true relations in N. In H, the substitutions are automorphisms, satisfy R, and hence define an A-action. The map f:H->N is equivariant and surjective. Peiffer identities on generators imply f(h) dot a = h a h^{-1} for every h,a in H, where dot denotes the left A-action. Thus if f(h)=1, Inn(h)=identity: ker f is central in H.

## Graded central-kernel lemma

For any surjection f:H->N with central kernel K, the kernel of gr_gamma(H)->gr_gamma(N) is contained in the center of gr_gamma(H). Indeed if x in gamma_c(H) maps into gamma_{c+1}(N), surjectivity gives x=z k for z in gamma_{c+1}(H),k in K. Then [x,H] is contained in gamma_{c+2}(H), proving its graded class brackets trivially with every degree-one class, hence with the entire generated graded Lie ring.

## Conditional all-degree torsion detector

Suppose a torsion class t in gr_d(H) admits iterated brackets t_k=[...[t,x_1],...,x_k] nonzero for arbitrarily large k, with each x_j in gr_1(H). Every t_k is torsion. If some t_k maps to zero in gr(N), then its centrality forces t_{k+1}=0 and all later brackets zero, a contradiction. Thus every t_k survives in gr(N). When N=IA_n, every torsion class maps to zero under the integral Johnson homomorphism, whose target is free abelian. This proves strictness in every corresponding degree.

The lemma does NOT by itself produce such a torsion tower or establish rank stabilization. Those are still required. It avoids the stronger and unnecessary demand that the finite invariant cover H be exactly IA_n or that all identities among GL_n quotient relators be known. Its construction is an invariant L-presentation with explicit finite seed relators and substitutions.

 import numpy as np,time
from pathlib import Path
D=9;LO=4
starts={d:sum(3**k for k in range(LO,d)) for d in range(LO,D+2)}
M=starts[D+1]
def deriv(A,B):
 # A,B shape(batch,3,M), output derivative D_A(B), truncated through9.
 A=np.asarray(A,dtype=np.int16);B=np.asarray(B,dtype=np.int16)
 n=len(A);out=np.zeros_like(A,dtype=np.int16)
 for d in range(4,7):
  for i in range(3):
   PP=B[:,i,starts[d]:starts[d+1]]
   if not PP.any():continue
   for pos in range(d):
    P=PP.reshape(n,3**pos,3,3**(d-pos-1))
    for j in range(3):
     R=P[:,:,j,:]
     if not R.any():continue
     for e in range(4,min(6,10-d)+1):
      Q=A[:,j,starts[e]:starts[e+1]]
      if not Q.any():continue
      out[:,i,starts[d+e-1]:starts[d+e]]+=np.einsum('nab,nc->nacb',R,Q,optimize=False).reshape(n,3**(d+e-1))
 return out%3

def logseed(f):return (np.asarray(f,dtype=np.int16)-2*deriv(f,f))%3
if __name__=='__main__':
 f=np.load('workspace/agent4_gamma3_d9_tensor_seeds.npz')['seeds']
 t=time.time();logs=logseed(f)
 assert np.array_equal(f,(logs+2*deriv(logs,logs))%3)
 np.savez_compressed('workspace/agent4_gamma3_d9_log_seeds.npz',seeds=logs.astype(np.int8))
 print('PASS log-exp inverse for all729 actualgamma3seeds; seconds',time.time()-t,flush=True)

 """Compact exact invariant-L-presentation seeds for central IA3 cover.
Signed-permutation chunks are shortened modulo explicitly imposed B3 relations.
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from degree2_certificate import compose,red,inv

def generate(limit=2000000):
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
 iw=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_inverse_words_n3.json'))
 wd=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_magnus_aut_words_n3.json'))
 ar=json.load(open('workspace/agent4_AutF3_four_generator_presentation.json'))
 order=['T','P','I','C'];mp={};aa={};ident=((1,),(2,),(3,))
 for i,s in enumerate(order,1):
  mp[i]=[tuple(w) for w in d['actions'][s]['words']];mp[-i]=[tuple(w) for w in iw[s]]
  aa[i]=tuple(map(tuple,d['actions'][s]['auto']));aa[-i]=tuple(map(tuple,d['actions'][s]['inverse']))
 def eva(w):
  f=ident
  for a in w:f=compose(f,aa[a])
  return f
 trans={ident:()};front=[ident]
 while front:
  new=[]
  for f in front:
   for a in (2,3,4,-4):
    h=compose(f,aa[a])
    if h not in trans:trans[h]=trans[f]+(a,);new.append(h)
  front=new
 assert len(trans)==48
 def normalize(w):
  out=();chunk=()
  for a in tuple(w):
   if abs(a)==1:
    out=red(out+trans[eva(chunk)]+(a,));chunk=()
   else:chunk+=(a,)
  return red(out+trans[eva(chunk)])
 def apply(a,w):
  out=red(y for x in w for y in (mp[a][x-1] if x>0 else inv(mp[a][-x-1])))
  if len(out)>limit:raise OverflowError(('wordcap',a,len(out)))
  return out
 def image(aw,h):
  w=(h+1,)
  for a in reversed(aw):w=apply(a,w)
  return w
 b3=[(2,2),(4,4,4),(2,4,2,4),(3,3),(3,2)*4]
 z=(4,2,-4);b3.append((3,)+z+(-3,)+inv(z))
 rel=[];lengths=[];operatorloops=list(b3);normalizedW=[]
 def addloop(aw):
  assert eva(aw)==ident
  for h in range(9):rel.append(red(image(aw,h)+(-h-1,)))
 for j,w in enumerate(b3):
  addloop(w);print('B3block',j,'maxlength',max(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 for j,w in enumerate(ar['presentation_loops_left_composition']):
  nw=normalize(w);assert eva(nw)==ident
  if nw:operatorloops.append(nw)
  addloop(nw)
  print('Autblock',j,'actionlength',len(nw),'maxword',max(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 for g,w in enumerate(wd['words']):
  aw=normalize(w);assert eva(aw)==tuple(map(tuple,d['generators'][g]))
  normalizedW.append(aw)
  for h in range(9):rel.append(red(image(aw,h)+(g+1,-h-1,-g-1)))
  print('Peifferblock',g,'maxword',max(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 out={'n':3,'endomorphisms':[mp[i] for i in (1,2,3,4,-1,-2,-3,-4)],'iterated_relators':rel,'fixed_relators':[],'aut_operator_relators':operatorloops,'magnus_aut_words_normalized':normalizedW,'proof':'B3 short presentation plus normalized complete Aut relators plus Peiffer; exact inverse endomorphisms included'}
 Path('workspace/agent2_compact_cover_seeds.json').write_text(json.dumps(out))
 print('Saved',len(rel),'relators totalletters',sum(map(len,rel)),flush=True)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':generate()
```

```
FILE agent2_lpres_cover.py 
 """Exact integral nilpotent quotients of the certified central IA3 cover.
This is NOT a presentation of IA3; central source artifacts may remain.
"""
import json,time,sys
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter
from sage.all import *
from sage.libs.gap.libgap import libgap

def build():
 assert bool(libgap.eval('LoadPackage("lpres",false:OnlyNeeded:=true)'))
 d=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_compact_cover_seeds.json'))
 F=libgap.FreeGroup(9);gen=list(libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(F))
 def word(w):
  q=libgap.One(F)
  for x in w:q=q*gen[abs(x)-1]**(1 if x>0 else -1)
  return q
 endos=[libgap.GroupHomomorphismByImages(F,F,gen,[word(w) for w in a]) for a in d['endomorphisms']]
 rels=[word(w) for w in d['iterated_relators'] if w]
 H=libgap.LPresentedGroup(F,[],endos,rels)
 return H

def run(c=3):
 H=build();t=time.time();Q=libgap.NilpotentQuotient(H,c)
 print('class',c,'integralNQtime',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 lcs=list(libgap.LowerCentralSeries(Q));invariants=[]
 for i in range(len(lcs)-1):
  inv=[int(x) for x in libgap.AbelianInvariants(libgap.FactorGroup(lcs[i],lcs[i+1]))]
  invariants.append(inv);print('LCSfactor',i+1,'counts',dict(Counter(inv)),flush=True)
 Path('workspace/agent2_cover_class%d_invariants.json'%c).write_text(json.dumps(invariants))
 return H,Q,invariants
if __name__=='__main__':run(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 3)

for v in reps:
 for i in range(3):
  out={}
  for w,c in zip(L3,v):

  assert not out
H=build();libgap.SetInfoLevel(libgap.InfoLPRES,1)

print('H4 computed seconds',time.time()-t,flush=True)
QS=libgap.NilpotentQuotientSystem(H);epi=QS['Epimorphism']
y=[libgap.Image(epi,x) for x in libgap.GeneratorsOfGroup(libgap.Source(epi))]
def comm(a,b):return a*b*a**-1*b**-1
@lru_cache(None)
def LW(w):
 if len(w)==1:return y[w[0]]
 k=next(k for k in range(1,len(w)) if lyndon(w[k:]))
 return comm(LW(w[:k]),LW(w[k:]))

beta=comm(comm(y[2],y[8]),y[7]);bc=[bool(comm(beta,x)==libgap.One(Q)) for x in y]

 for w,c in zip(L3,v):
  if c:a=a*LW(w)**int(c)
 row=[bool(comm(a,x)==libgap.One(Q)) for x in y]
 checks.append(row);print('torsionrep',k,'centrality',row,flush=True)
L=list(libgap.LowerCentralSeriesOfGroup(Q));invs=[]

 iv=list(map(int,libgap.AbelianInvariants(libgap.FactorGroup(L[i],L[i+1]))));invs.append(iv)
 print('actual layer',i+1,Counter(iv),flush=True)
```

```
 import sys,time
import numpy as np
from itertools import product
from agent3_magnus import K,C,auto_commutator

def expand(w,d=8,p=3):
 starts=[(3**k-1)//2 for k in range(d+2)]
 A=np.zeros(starts[d+1],dtype=np.int8);A[0]=1
 for a in w:
  i=abs(a)-1
  for k in (range(d,0,-1) if a>0 else range(1,d+1)):
   # All words of length k with final letter i, versus their prefixes.
   dest=slice(starts[k]+i,starts[k+1],3);src=slice(starts[k-1],starts[k])
   A[dest]=(A[dest]+(1 if a>0 else -1)*A[src])%p
 return A

def seeds():
 G=[]
 for i in range(1,4):
  for j in range(1,4):
   if i!=j:G.append(K(3,i,j))
  js=[j for j in range(1,4) if j!=i];G.append(C(3,i,*js))
 G2=[]
 for (a,ai),(b,bi) in product(G,repeat=2):
  h=auto_commutator(a,ai,b,bi);hi=auto_commutator(b,bi,a,ai);G2.append((h,hi))
 G3=[]
 for (a,ai),(b,bi) in product(G2,G):G3.append(auto_commutator(a,ai,b,bi))
 N=(3**9-1)//2
 out=np.zeros((len(G3),3,N),dtype=np.int8)
 cache={};t=time.time()
 for k,g in enumerate(G3):
  for i,w in enumerate(g):
   if w not in cache:cache[w]=expand(w)
   out[k,i]=cache[w]
   out[k,i,0]-=1;out[k,i,1+i]-=1;out[k,i]%=3
  assert not out[k,:,:40].any() # no displacement degree<4
  if k%100==0:print(k,'elapsed',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 np.savez_compressed('workspace/agent3_gamma3_tensor_seeds.npz',seeds=out[:,:,(3**4-1)//2:])
 print('saved',out.shape,'elapsed',time.time()-t)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':seeds()

fun,args=matrix(GF(3),[[1]]).__reduce__()
def fastmat(A):
 a=list(args);a[1]=MatrixSpace(GF(3),*A.shape);a[4]=(1,True,A.astype(np.uint8,copy=False).tobytes());return fun(*a)
def asnumpy(M):return np.frombuffer(M.__reduce__()[1][4][2],dtype=np.uint8).reshape(M.nrows(),M.ncols())
def echelon(A):
 M=fastmat(A);M.echelonize();return asnumpy(M[:M.rank(),:])

FILE agent3_magnus.py 
 """Exact reduced-word and truncated integral Magnus computations.
No numerical linear algebra or inferred identities.  Words use signed 1-based letters.
"""
from collections import defaultdict

def reduce_word(w):
    out=[]
    for a in w:
        if out and out[-1]==-a: out.pop()
        else: out.append(a)
    return tuple(out)

def inverse(w): return tuple(-a for a in reversed(w))
def product(*ws): return reduce_word(a for w in ws for a in w)
def substitute(w, images):
    return product(*(images[a-1] if a>0 else inverse(images[-a-1]) for a in w))
def compose(f,g): return tuple(substitute(w,f) for w in g)
def auto_commutator(f,fi,g,gi): return compose(compose(compose(f,g),fi),gi)
def word_commutator(u,v): return product(u,v,inverse(u),inverse(v))
def identity(n): return tuple((i,) for i in range(1,n+1))
def multiply(a,b,degree):
    out=defaultdict(int)
    for u,x in a.items():
        for v,y in b.items():
            if len(u)+len(v)<=degree: out[u+v]+=x*y
    return {w:c for w,c in out.items() if c}
def magnus(w,degree):
    out={():1}
    for a in w:
        i=abs(a)
        factor={():1,(i,):1} if a>0 else {(i,)*k:(-1)**k for k in range(degree+1)}
        out=multiply(out,factor,degree)
    return out

def johnson_terms(images,degree):
    """Return Magnus expansions of f(x_i)x_i^-1, less constant 1, through degree."""
    out=[]
    for i,w in enumerate(images,1):
        q=magnus(product(w,(-i,)),degree)
        q.pop((),None)
        out.append(q)
    return tuple(out)
def is_andreadakis(images,r):
    """Exact test f in A_r, assuming images define an automorphism.
    Magnus dimension theorem over Z: w in gamma_(r+1) iff terms <=r vanish.
    """
    return not any(johnson_terms(images,r))
def K(n,i,j):
    f=list(identity(n)); f[i-1]=(j,i,-j)
    fi=list(identity(n)); fi[i-1]=(-j,i,j)
    return tuple(f),tuple(fi)
def C(n,i,j,k):
    f=list(identity(n)); h=word_commutator((j,),(k,)); f[i-1]=product((i,),h)
    fi=list(identity(n)); fi[i-1]=product((i,),inverse(h))
    return tuple(f),tuple(fi)

def self_test():
    for n in range(2,6):
        gens=[]
        for i in range(1,n+1):
            for j in range(1,n+1):
                if i!=j: gens.append(K(n,i,j))
            for j in range(1,n+1):
                for k in range(j+1,n+1):
                    if i not in (j,k): gens.append(C(n,i,j,k))
        for f,fi in gens:
            assert compose(f,fi)==compose(fi,f)==identity(n)
            assert is_andreadakis(f,1) and not is_andreadakis(f,2)
        # Every pair is checked, not sampled.
        for f,fi in gens:
            for g,gi in gens:
                h=auto_commutator(f,fi,g,gi)
                assert is_andreadakis(h,2)
        print('n=',n,'generators=',len(gens),'pair-commutators checked=',len(gens)**2)
    # A genuine nonzero degree-two Johnson image.
    f,fi=K(3,1,2); g,gi=K(3,2,3)
    h=auto_commutator(f,fi,g,gi)
    assert not is_andreadakis(h,3)
    print('nonzero degree-two leading terms:',johnson_terms(h,3))
if __name__=='__main__': self_test()
```

```
"""Memory-efficient exact F3 rowspace via GAP CVEC (3 bits/coefficient).
NumPy exchange uses portable CVEC external packed32-bit words, 10coeffs/word.
Binary IO uses GAP IO_Write, not PrintTo (which strips NULs).
"""
from sage.all import *
from sage.libs.gap.libgap import libgap
import numpy as np
libgap.eval('LoadPackage("cvec",false:OnlyNeeded:=true)')
libgap.eval('LoadPackage("io",false:OnlyNeeded:=true)')
EXPORT=libgap.eval('''function(B,filename) local f,v,s; f:=IO_File(filename,"w"); for v in B!.vectors do s:=""; CVEC_CVEC_TO_EXTREP(v,s); IO_Write(f,s); od; IO_Close(f); return B!.pivots;end''')
INFO=libgap.eval('function(B)return [Length(B!.vectors),B!.pivots];end')
ESCAPES=['\\%03o'%b for b in range(256)]
class CVecBasis:
 def __init__(self,n):
  self.n=n;self.cl=libgap.CVecClass(3,1,n)
  self.basis=libgap.EmptySemiEchelonBasis(libgap.CMat([],self.cl))
 def insert(self,row):
  a=np.asarray(row,dtype=np.uint32)%3
  if len(a)!=self.n:raise ValueError('wrong length')
  a=np.pad(a,(0,(-len(a))%10))
  packed=(a.reshape(-1,10)<<np.arange(0,30,3,dtype=np.uint32)).sum(axis=1,dtype=np.uint32).astype('<u4').tobytes()
  s=libgap.eval('"'+''.join(ESCAPES[b] for b in packed)+'"')
  v=libgap.CVec(self.cl);libgap.CVEC_EXTREP_TO_CVEC(s,v)
  return not bool(libgap.CleanRow(self.basis,v,True,libgap.fail))
 def info(self):return INFO(self.basis).sage()
 def export(self,filename):
  piv=EXPORT(self.basis,filename).sage();return piv
 def numpy(self,filename):
  piv=self.export(filename);raw=np.fromfile(filename,dtype='<u4').reshape(len(piv),-1)
  A=((raw[:,:,None]>>np.arange(0,30,3,dtype=np.uint32))&7).astype(np.uint8).reshape(len(piv),-1)[:,:self.n]
  assert np.all(A<3)
  return A,piv
```

```
from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix,save_npz,load_npz

from agent3_magnus import K,C,magnus,inverse,product as wp
D=8
WORDS=[w for d in range(1,D+1) for w in product(range(3),repeat=d)]
IX={w:j for j,w in enumerate(WORDS)};M=len(WORDS)
def mul(P,Q):
 o=defaultdict(int)
 for w,c in P.items():
  for v,e in Q.items():
   if len(w)+len(v)<=D:o[w+v]+=c*e
 return {w:c%3 for w,c in o.items() if c%3}
def build():
 G=[]
 for i in range(1,4):
  for j in range(1,4):
   if i!=j:G.append(K(3,i,j))
  other=[j for j in range(1,4) if j!=i];G.append(C(3,i,*other))
 ops={}
 for j,pair in enumerate(G):
  for sign,im in zip([1,-1],pair):
   fname=f'workspace/agent1_scalar_S_{j}_{sign}.npz'
   if os.path.exists(fname):ops[sign*(j+1)]=load_npz(fname);continue
   polys=[]
   for w in im:
    P=magnus(w,D);P.pop((),None)
    polys.append({tuple(a-1 for a in v):c%3 for v,c in P.items() if c%3})
   cache={():{():1}};rows=[];cols=[];vals=[];t=time.time()
   for k,w in enumerate(WORDS):
    P=mul(cache[w[:-1]],polys[w[-1]]);cache[w]=P
    for v,c in P.items():rows.append(IX[v]);cols.append(k);vals.append(c)
   S=coo_matrix((np.array(vals,dtype=np.int32),(rows,cols)),shape=(M,M)).tocsr()
   save_npz(fname,S);ops[sign*(j+1)]=S
   print('built scalar',j,sign,'nnz',S.nnz,'seconds',time.time()-t,flush=True)
 return ops
def comm(u,v):return wp(u,v,inverse(u),inverse(v))
def audit():
 ops=build()
 E=np.zeros((M,3),dtype=np.int32);E[:3]=np.eye(3,dtype=np.int32)
 for j in range(1,10):assert np.array_equal((ops[j]@(ops[-j]@E))%3,E)
 source=json.load(open('workspace/agent1_d8_121_seed_provenance.json'))
 target=np.load('workspace/agent3_gamma4_tensor_seedmatrix.npy')

 results=[]
 for row,(k,g) in source.items():
  a=k//81;b=k//9%9;c=k%9
  w=comm(comm(comm((a+1,),(b+1,)),(c+1,)),(-g-1,))
  V=E.copy()
  for s in reversed(w):V=(ops[s]@V)%3
  V=(V-E)%3
  assert not V[:120].any()
  got=V[ix].T.reshape(-1).astype(np.uint8)
  ok=np.array_equal(got,target[int(row)])
  results.append(ok)
  if not ok:
   print('FAIL',row,k,g,'mismatches',np.count_nonzero(got!=target[int(row)]),flush=True)
   np.save('workspace/agent1_d8_failed_actual.npy',got)
   break
 print('literal scalar-matrix audit',sum(results),'of',len(results),'PASS',all(results),flush=True)
 json.dump(results,open('workspace/agent1_d8_121_scalar_checks.json','w'))
if __name__=='__main__':audit()
''')
```

```
Requires NumPy, SciPy, Sage/libgap CVEC (helper code agent3_cvec_basis.py).
Scalar matrices are computed from nine actual IA3 generator words.
"""
import sys,time,json,numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix,kron,eye
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent1_scalar_magnus_audit import WORDS,IX,M,build,comm
from agent3_cvec_basis import CVecBasis

def actions(ops):
 ix=[j for j,w in enumerate(WORDS) if len(w)>=5]
 words=[WORDS[j] for j in ix];wi={w:j for j,w in enumerate(words)};m=len(words)
 Ns=[]
 for g in range(9):
  S=ops[g+1];Sinv=ops[-g-1][ix,:][:,ix]
  rr=[];cc=[];vals=[]
  for i in range(3):
   col=S[:,i].tocoo()
   for vv,coef in zip(col.row,col.data):
    v=WORDS[vv]
    for pos,j in enumerate(v):
     for w in words:
      if len(v)+len(w)>9:break
      u=v[:pos]+w+v[pos+1:]
      rr.append(i*m+wi[u]);cc.append(j*m+wi[w]);vals.append(int(coef))
  J=coo_matrix((np.array(vals,dtype=np.int32),(rr,cc)),shape=(3*m,3*m)).tocsr()
  A=(kron(eye(3,dtype=np.int32),Sinv,format='csr')@J-eye(3*m,dtype=np.int32)).tocsr()
  A.data%=3;A.eliminate_zeros();Ns.append(A)
 return Ns

def images(N,B,chunk=64):
 for j in range(0,len(B),chunk):
  for row in ((N@B[j:j+chunk].T).T%3).astype(np.uint8):yield row

def main():
 t=time.time();ops=build();Ns=actions(ops)
 E=np.zeros((M,3),dtype=np.int32);E[:3]=np.eye(3,dtype=np.int32)
 for g in range(1,10):assert np.array_equal((ops[g]@(ops[-g]@E))%3,E)
 B=CVecBasis(29160);nseeds=0
 for a in range(1,10):
  for b in range(1,10):
   for c in range(1,10):
    for g in range(1,10):
     w=comm(comm(comm((a,),(b,)),(c,)),(-g,))
     V=E.copy()
     for s in reversed(w):V=(ops[s]@V)%3
     V=(V-E)%3
     assert not V[:120].any()
     B.insert(V[120:].T.reshape(-1));nseeds+=1
  print('seeds processed',nseeds,'rank',B.info()[0],flush=True)
 r4=B.info()[0];A,piv=B.numpy('workspace/agent1_direct_d8_L4.packed')
 for g,N in enumerate(Ns):
  for row in images(N,A):assert not B.insert(row),('L4invariance',g)
  print('L4invariance',g,'PASS',flush=True)
 S=CVecBasis(29160)
 for g,N in enumerate(Ns):
  for row in images(N,A):S.insert(row)
  print('L5generator block',g,'rank',S.info()[0],flush=True)
 r5=S.info()[0];C,piv=S.numpy('workspace/agent1_direct_d8_L5.packed')
 for g,N in enumerate(Ns):
  for row in images(N,C):assert not S.insert(row),('L5invariance',g)
  print('L5invariance',g,'PASS',flush=True)
 out={'gamma4_dimension':r4,'gamma5_dimension':r5,'quotient_dimension':r4-r5,'actual_simple_commutators_checked':nseeds,'seconds':time.time()-t,'all_invariance_checks':True}
 json.dump(out,open('workspace/agent1_direct_d8_certificate.json','w'))
 print(out,flush=True)
 return out
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')
```

```
from itertools import product

D=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_nielsen_actions_n3.json'))
gens={i+1:tuple(map(tuple,g)) for i,g in enumerate(D['generators'])}
gens.update({-i-1:tuple(map(tuple,g)) for i,g in enumerate(D['inverses'])})
def comm(a,b):return reduce_word(a+b+inverse(a)+inverse(b))
C=json.load(open('workspace/agent4_explicit_degree4_kernel_candidate.json'))
word=()
for _,sgn,(a,b,c,g) in C['term_indices']:
 t=comm(comm(comm((a+1,),(b+1,)),(c+1,)),(-g-1,))
 word+=t if sgn>0 else inverse(t)
word=reduce_word(word)
print('explicit candidate reducedF9length',len(word))
words=[w for l in range(1,6) for w in product(range(3),repeat=l)];ix={w:i for i,w in enumerate(words)}
def mul(A,B):
 P=defaultdict(int)
 for u,c in A.items():
  for v,d in B.items():
   if len(u)+len(v)<=5:P[u+v]+=c*d
 return {w:c for w,c in P.items() if c}

 G=[]
 for w in g:

 cache={():{():1}};rr=[];cc=[];vv=[]

 S[a]=coo_matrix((np.array(vv,dtype=np.int64),(rr,cc)),shape=(len(words),len(words))).tocsr()
V=np.zeros((len(words),3),dtype=np.int64)

start=V.copy();peak=0
```

```
import json
from pathlib import Path
terms=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_W_detector_functional.json'))['terms']
functional=[(t['output'],''.join(map(str,t['word'])),t['coefficient']) for t in terms]
code='''"""Self-contained NumPy/SciPy finite-Magnus certificate.
All IA3 images, scalar substitutions, conjugation operators and witnesses
are constructed here. No stored matrices, precomputed ranks, Sage or GAP.
"""
import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix,csr_matrix,kron,eye,vstack
from itertools import product
from collections import defaultdict
import time

def inv(w):return tuple(-a for a in reversed(w))
def comm(u,v):return u+v+inv(u)+inv(v)
def gens():
 G={}
 for i in range(1,4):
  for j in range(1,4):
   if i==j:continue
   a=[(k,) for k in range(1,4)];b=a.copy()
   a[i-1]=(j,i,-j);b[i-1]=(-j,i,j)
   n=len(G)//2+1;G[n]=a;G[-n]=b
  j,k=[k for k in range(1,4) if k!=i]
  a=[(k,) for k in range(1,4)];b=a.copy()
  a[i-1]=(i,j,k,-j,-k);b[i-1]=(i,k,j,-k,-j)
  n=len(G)//2+1;G[n]=a;G[-n]=b
 return G

def scalar(D,p):
 words=[w for d in range(1,D+1) for w in product(range(3),repeat=d)]
 IX={w:j for j,w in enumerate(words)};M=len(words)
 def clean(P):return {w:(c%p if p else c) for w,c in P.items() if (c%p if p else c)}
 def mul(P,Q):
  R=defaultdict(int)
  for u,c in P.items():
   for v,e in Q.items():
    if len(u)+len(v)<=D:R[u+v]+=c*e
  return clean(R)
 def magnus(w):
  P={():1}
  for a in w:
   i=abs(a)-1
   Q={():1,(i,):1} if a>0 else {(i,)*k:(-1)**k for k in range(D+1)}
   P=mul(P,Q)
  P.pop((),None);return P
 S={}
 for a,g in gens().items():
  G=[magnus(w) for w in g]
  cache={():{():1}};rr=[];cc=[];vv=[]
  for j,w in enumerate(words):
   P=mul(cache[w[:-1]],G[w[-1]]);cache[w]=P
   for v,c in P.items():rr.append(IX[v]);cc.append(j);vv.append(c)
  S[a]=coo_matrix((np.array(vv,dtype=np.int64),(rr,cc)),shape=(M,M)).tocsr()
 E=np.zeros((M,3),dtype=np.int64);E[:3]=np.eye(3,dtype=np.int64)
 return S,E,words

def evaluate(word,S,E,p):
 V=E.copy()
 for a in reversed(word):
  V=S[a]@V
  if p:V%=p
 return V-E

def adjoints(S,words):
 D=max(map(len,words));inds=[j for j,w in enumerate(words) if len(w)>=5]
 ws=[words[j] for j in inds];IX={w:j for j,w in enumerate(ws)};m=len(ws)
 degree=np.array([len(w) for w in ws]*3)
 N=[]
 for g in range(1,10):
  Sinv=S[-g][inds,:][:,inds];rr=[];cc=[];vv=[]
  for i in range(3):
   P=S[g][:,i].tocoo()
   for a,c in zip(P.row,P.data):
    v=words[a]
    for pos,j in enumerate(v):
     for w in ws:
      if len(v)+len(w)>D+1:break
      u=v[:pos]+w+v[pos+1:]
      rr.append(i*m+IX[u]);cc.append(j*m+IX[w]);vv.append(int(c))
  J=coo_matrix((np.array(vv,dtype=np.int64),(rr,cc)),shape=(3*m,3*m)).tocsr()
  A=(kron(eye(3,dtype=np.int64),Sinv,format='csr')@J-eye(3*m,dtype=np.int64)).tocsr()
  A.data%=3;A.eliminate_zeros()
  rows,cols=A.nonzero();assert np.all(degree[rows]>degree[cols])
  N.append(A)
 return N,inds,ws

FUNCTIONAL = '''+repr(functional)+'''

def main():
 t=time.time()
 # One-based Magnus generator indices, inverse fourth argument.
 def T(a,b,c,g):return comm(comm(comm((a,),(b,)),(c,)),(-g,))
 W=T(1,9,4,2)+T(1,9,4,5)+T(5,4,9,1)+inv(T(5,8,9,4))
 # Integral, not mod3, verification W lies in A5.
 SZ,EZ,wordsZ=scalar(5,0)
 assert not evaluate(W,SZ,EZ,0).any()
 print('PASS: integral Magnus action of W through degree5 is identity',flush=True)
 S,E,words=scalar(7,3)
 for g in range(1,10):assert not evaluate((g,-g),S,E,3).any()
 N,inds,ws=adjoints(S,words);m=len(ws);IX={w:j for j,w in enumerate(ws)}
 lam=np.zeros(3*m,dtype=np.int64)
 for out,w,c in FUNCTIONAL:lam[out*m+IX[tuple(map(int,w))]]=c
 # N raises degree; on degrees5..7 every product of three N is zero.
 lam=csr_matrix(lam.reshape(1,-1))
 first=[lam@A for A in N]
 forms=vstack(first+[f@A for f in first for A in N],format='csr')
 forms.data%=3;forms.eliminate_zeros()
 checked=0
 for a,b,c,g in product(range(1,10),repeat=4):
  V=evaluate(T(a,b,c,g),S,E,3)
  assert not V[:inds[0]].any()
  v=V[inds].T.reshape(-1)
  assert not ((forms@v)%3).any(),(a,b,c,g)
  checked+=1
 # Hence lambda vanishes on all gamma5 images by normal generation.
 V=evaluate(W,S,E,3);assert not V[:inds[0]].any()
 value=int((lam@V[inds].T.reshape(-1))[0]%3)
 assert value!=0
 print('PASS:',checked,'actual weight4 seeds annihilated by all90 dual forms',flush=True)
 print('PASS: lambda(W)=',value,'mod3; therefore W not in gamma5(IA3)',flush=True)
 print('seconds',time.time()-t,flush=True)
if __name__=='__main__':main()
'''
Path('workspace/agent1_compact_D7_conflict_certificate.py').write_text(code)
import sys;sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent1_compact_D7_conflict_certificate import main
main()
```
